  Issue [#2463](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2463); Pull request [#2481](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2481); Commit [`b90ce9e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b90ce9eb8c7cd9e9b131477a377249a3d3fda181)

### v2.54.0 Release Notes

*Target Time for Versioned Storage*

*Released October 21, 2024*

**NOTE TO PACKAGERS**: This is last feature release to support the autoconf/make build. Please migrate to meson if you have not already done so. 2.54.X patch releases (if any) will continue to support autoconf/make.

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix PostgreSQL query performance for large datasets. (*Fixed by Thibault Vincent, David Steele. Reviewed by David Christensen. Tested by Antoine Millet. Reported by Antoine Millet.*)

  The query timeout logic delayed reading query results, which could make a query that returned a large result take up to `db-timeout`. Results are now read as soon as they are available.

  [`6ac4e8d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6ac4e8dd78f16e93300f7747c4bc8fa8e1a0558b) Move fd module shim to io module test.

  [`77ae753`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/77ae753ef59bd40b9867be21e8519093786c3b85) Allow fdReady() shim to be run only once with a specified return value.

  [`33fa396`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/33fa3965616b1432e5d1d59c489302fa4891a39e) Refactor Wait object to expose remaining wait as waitRemains().

  [`c8ccaaa`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c8ccaaa755993e3fd5e87148bd0a991acb3050c3) Fix PostgreSQL query performance for large datasets.; Issue [#2458](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2458); Pull request [#2459](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2459)

**Features:**

- Allow repositories on versioned storage to be read at a target time. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot, David Christensen.*)

  The `archive-get`, `info`, `repo-get`, `repo-ls`, `restore`, and `verify` commands can read a repository on versioned Azure, GCS, or S3 storage as it was at `repo-target-time`. This allows recovery even when the repository has been deleted or corrupted, accidentally or maliciously.

  Pull request [#2404](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2404); Commit [`b19134d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b19134dccd5d0dab87b5d45b443f481df00370b1)
- Allow requested standby backup to proceed with no standby. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  Set `backup-standby=prefer` to back up from a standby when one is available and from the primary otherwise. This does not help when the standby responds but fails to sync with the primary after the backup starts.

  Pull request [#2378](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2378); Commit [`a42629f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a42629f87ab70ffaee8d1241eb50c5ea7154a87d)

**Improvements:**

- Summarize backup reference list for `info` command text output. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The backup reference list can be very long, so the text output of the `info` command now summarizes it. The full list is shown when `--set` is specified.

  Pull request [#2399](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2399); Commit [`b306f83`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b306f83493cc859ad15c8f175fc6db2321b79340)
- Refresh web-id token for each S3 authentication. (*Contributed by Brent Graveland. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The token file named by `AWS_WEB_IDENTITY_TOKEN_FILE` was read once at startup, so the token could expire during a long operation. The token is now read for each authentication.

  Issue [#2428](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2428); Pull request [#2429](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2429); Commit [`95a4b9e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/95a4b9e10a3f7a26ebaae22fc6abd79cee14189a)
- Correctly display current values for indexed options in help. (*Reviewed by David Christensen.*)

  The current value shown for an indexed option was always the value for the first index, e.g. `pg1-path`. The option help now lists the value for each index, and the option list shows `<multi>` when there is more than one.

  Pull request [#2453](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2453); Commit [`b3ca2e3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b3ca2e34823b28862d999f87c4d7346707952a5b)
- Save `backup.info` only when contents have changed. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  `expire` saved `backup.info` even when it had not changed. Fewer saves also make it easier to choose a target time on versioned storage.

  Pull request [#2403](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2403); Commit [`b454b9e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b454b9e403526b1f352df964d9206c27d2397df3)
- Remove limitation on reading files in parallel during restore. (*Reviewed by David Christensen.*)

  The protocol could only read or write one file at a time on a remote, so a restore read one file at a time from the repository. Protocol commands can now be multiplexed, so a restore can read more than one file at a time, e.g. a bundle and the block incremental data for a file in it.

  [`df8cbc9`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/df8cbc91c348bc2f746767c87ac5626460fb6f39) Protocol command multiplexing.; Pull request [#2108](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2108)

  [`46c1297`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/46c1297be344909e900be596a865064233da9f87) Remove limitation on reading files in parallel during restore.; Pull request [#2436](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2436)
- Improve SFTP error messages. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  SFTP errors were reported as numbers only. They now include a description of the error.

  Pull request [#2439](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2439); Commit [`70bda2c`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/70bda2cfb21130eb589a87cb419fb5f22a71c78a)

**Documentation Features:**

- Add performance tuning section to user guide. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  The options that commonly improve performance were spread through the documentation. The quickstart now has a section that lists them.

  Pull request [#2446](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2446); Commit [`278319e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/278319eba3444070123d3f0f277be9234604d937)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Clarify source for `data_directory`. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele. Suggested by Matthias.*)

  `pg-path` should match the `data_directory` reported by PostgreSQL, which may differ from the value in `postgresql.conf`.

  Issue [#2464](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2464); Pull request [#2467](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2467); Commit [`9ba2db0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9ba2db017348d304f96ce89592fc01f29197e136)
- Better logic for deciding when a summary should be lower-cased. (*Suggested by Daniel Westermann.*)

  Summaries that start with a proper name or an acronym, e.g. PostgreSQL, are no longer lower-cased in the documentation.

  Issue [#2462](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2462); Commit [`eb2f279`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/eb2f279a2998d1ed893c5d8984590ab4997a5f32)

### v2.53.1 Release Notes

*PostgreSQL 17 Support*

*Released August 19, 2024*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix permissions when `restore` run as root user. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Reported by Will M.*)

  When `restore` was run as root, `pg_control` was owned by root. A restore as root also failed when the base path did not exist.

  Issue [#2407](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2407); Pull request [#2416](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2416); Commit [`7bd8da4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7bd8da4eecb0ce1461854414b727606a5e884edf)
- Fix segfault on delayed connection errors. (*Reviewed by David Christensen. Reported by Anton Glushakov.*)

  A connection error that was delayed long enough to be reported on a later wait could cause a segfault.

  Issue [#2420](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2420); Pull request [#2422](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2422); Commit [`377ba06`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/377ba062ac81867dca6197265688deb0111ae81a)
- Skip local repository duplicate check for SFTP. (*Fixed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele. Reported by Anton Kurochkin.*)

  The check for local repositories that are configured more than once was also applied to SFTP repositories, which are never local.

  Issue [#2418](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2418); Pull request [#2421](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2421); Commit [`9840304`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/98403046ed6af4c54fa2f572ecf9c211f94b67db)

**Improvements:**

- PostgreSQL 17 support.

  PostgreSQL 17beta3 changed the control and WAL formats, which is unusual for a beta.

  Commit [`189d15d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/189d15d1a640e31aac5ffbbd0fb06eb9eed8b576)

### v2.53 Release Notes

*Concurrent Backups*

*Released July 22, 2024*

**IMPORTANT NOTE**: The `log-level-stderr` option default has been changed from `warn` to `off`. This makes it easier to capture errors when only redirecting `stdout`. To preserve the prior behavior set `log-level-stderr=warn`.<br/> <br/> **NOTE TO PACKAGERS**: The lz4 library is now required by the meson build.<br/> <br/> **NOTE TO PACKAGERS**: Compiler support for `__builtin_clzl()` and `__builtin_bswap64()` is now required by the meson build.

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix SFTP renaming failure when file already exists. (*Fixed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele. Reported by ahmed112212.*)

  Some SFTP servers report that a file already exists with an error that was not handled, so renaming a file over an existing file failed.

  Issue [#2376](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2376); Pull request [#2377](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2377); Commit [`d6f0bf8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/d6f0bf88af8b39492aa8e20c09bdf30260e2eb2f)

**Features:**

- Allow backups to run concurrently on different repositories. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson, Stefan Fercot.*)

  Backups of the same stanza can now run at the same time on different repositories, since locks are now per stanza and repository instead of per stanza. This is not possible for PostgreSQL <= 9.5.

  [`270dce4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/270dce41b6fff0d0ad0769b0ea5df0011140f0b9) Refactor lock module.; Pull request [#2371](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2371)

  [`cfb8aa2`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cfb8aa202ee972579697db2cc8d5d8ef111f0022) Add remote locks for stanza commands missed in 31c7824a.

  [`edd6163`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/edd61636a98d193b7c0634b5a4444527132959e0) Allow backups to run concurrently on different repositories.; Pull request [#2389](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2389)
- Support IP-based SANs for TLS certificate validation. (*Contributed by David Christensen. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Certificates with an IP address as the subject alternative name, e.g. `IP:1.2.3.4`, are now validated. IPv4 and IPv6 addresses must match exactly.

  Issue [#1977](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1977); Pull request [#2047](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2047); Commit [`dfb620b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/dfb620b0b8cf884ca486b413f4cd162e258b2fc5)

**Improvements:**

- Default `log-level-stderr` option to `off`. (*Reviewed by Greg Sabino Mullane, Stefan Fercot.*)

  Warnings and errors written to stderr were often lost when only stdout was redirected while collecting logs. An explicitly set `log-level-stderr` is not affected.

  Pull request [#2379](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2379); Commit [`7d54073`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7d540730941994d3cb630c695e9f672e047d8e7f)
- Allow alternative WAL segment sizes for PostgreSQL ≤ 10. (*Contributed by Viktor Kurilko. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  PostgreSQL <= 10 can be built with a different WAL segment size, and forks such as Greenplum set it with `initdb`.

  Pull request [#2303](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2303); Commit [`4ac3b82`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/4ac3b82c99ff3bd48d2cc74651a47ce072ac0022)
- Add hint to check SFTP authorization log. (*Contributed by Vitalii Zurian. Reviewed by Reid Thompson, David Steele.*)

  When SFTP authentication fails the error now hints that the authorization log on the server may have more detail.

  Issue [#2381](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2381); Pull request [#2385](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2385); Commit [`ccae609`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ccae60940241b30e8bc3e405936b6ba68857930c)
- Display time option defaults and allowed values with appropriate units.

  Time option defaults and allowed values were shown in seconds, which was hard to read for large values. Fractional seconds are no longer allowed, so a value such as `archive-timeout=0.5` must be written as `archive-timeout=500ms`.

  Commit [`3a2266f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3a2266f327c340ff1133a6a2398429c844b5755d)
- Require OpenSSL >= 1.1.1.

  Commit [`cf8625d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cf8625d24bd953ad1ed448c32b937584009e5b6b)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Clarify `archive-push` multi-repo behavior. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  When one repository is failing, `archive-push` cannot push ahead to the other repositories unless `archive-async` is enabled.

  Pull request [#2398](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2398); Commit [`ec3e387`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ec3e387bb73826c30b32bf0221acb2d56f23f1c5)

### v2.52.1 Release Notes

*Bug Fix*

*Released June 25, 2024*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix issue with files larger on the replica than on the primary. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Reported by Nicolas Lassimonne.*)

  With block incremental, when a file was a different size on the primary than on the standby during a backup from standby, the next diff or incr backup stored the size from the primary. The backup after that failed with "repo size must be > 0".

  [`83aaa86`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/83aaa868ef396348bf96589457f13c1805b25cb6) Add unit tests for backup from standby with block incremental.

  [`aabc122`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/aabc122021a582e9d0193f978cf710cff95c2bee) Fix issue with files larger on the replica than on the primary.; Issue [#2358](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2358); Pull request [#2367](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2367)

### v2.52 Release Notes

*PostgreSQL 17beta1 Support*

*Released May 27, 2024*

**NOTE TO PACKAGERS**: The build system for pgBackRest is now meson. The autoconf/make build will not receive any new features and will be removed after a few releases.

**Features:**

- Add GCS batch delete support. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson.*)

  Files are removed with batch requests instead of one request per file, which speeds up `expire` and other commands that remove files from the repository.

  Pull request [#2326](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2326); Commit [`76bcb74`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/76bcb740b66f64804dbbf5a2a6be6407f9cec376)
- S3 `SSE-C` encryption support. (*Reviewed by Tim Jones. Suggested by Tim Jones.*)

  Set `repo-s3-sse-customer-key` to have S3 encrypt the data with the provided key after it is transmitted. This is not as secure as encrypting the data before it is transmitted with `repo-cipher-type`.

  Issue [#2279](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2279); Pull request [#2282](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2282); Commit [`57731b6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/57731b6cd261c732041aacc094429ab02ca13418)
- PostgreSQL 17beta1 support. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  Adds the catalog version and WAL magic number for PostgreSQL 17.

  Pull request [#2328](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2328); Commit [`fb22f04`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/fb22f04555fecfa143354372ec3543ab186c131d)

**Improvements:**

- Allow explicit disabling of optional dependencies in meson builds. (*Contributed by Michael Schout. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  An optional feature can be disabled in the meson build even when its library is installed, as some platforms require, e.g. FreeBSD. By default a feature is enabled when its library is found.

  Pull request [#2321](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2321); Commit [`7b95fd3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7b95fd3bd29346e7325aa161bfee45efd71e22eb)
- Dynamically find python in meson build. (*Contributed by Michael Schout. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The meson build now finds the python installation instead of requiring a `python3` binary on the path, e.g. when only `python3.9` is installed. The minimum meson version is now 0.47.

  [`55e9969`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/55e996912aa57d39ea21f5dc905b7acabe93b0f8) Update meson minimum version to 0.47.

  [`e00e33b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e00e33b52856a63e82552ccc12df235d1a7f6f17) Dynamically find python in meson build.; Pull request [#2338](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2338)
- Tag `pgbackrest` build target in meson as installable. (*Contributed by Bradford Boyle. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Pull request [#2312](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2312); Commit [`7877983`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7877983acb3b1a27e900f7e7aea89cb7b29a817d)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Update `start`/`stop` documentation to reflect actual functionality. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  Issue [#2325](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2325); Issue [#2334](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2334); Pull request [#2352](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2352); Commit [`b6ac11b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b6ac11beec920fe978994c0d018c380477199ca8)

### v2.51 Release Notes

*Meson Build System*

*Released March 25, 2024*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Skip zero-length files for block incremental delta restore. (*Reviewed by Sebastian Krause, René Højbjerg Larsen. Reported by Sebastian Krause.*)

  An existing zero-length file caused a block incremental delta restore to fail in the block checksum filter. These files are now skipped, since there is nothing in them to reuse.

  Issue [#2271](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2271); Pull request [#2272](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2272); Commit [`f7a7ab1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f7a7ab16c9652179945717ebf9b68af3aa8a0b0e)
- Fix performance regression in storage list. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Reported by Maksym Boguk.*)

  Listing a path requested detailed information for each file, which could be slow, e.g. on object stores. Only the information the list needs is now requested.

  Issue [#2275](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2275); Pull request [#2276](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2276); Commit [`e00bfe2`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e00bfe2d2c3f124b2883e3c2d169a2ce506a1e98)
- Fix progress logging when file size changes during backup. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Reported by samkingno.*)

  Issue [#2298](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2298); Pull request [#2304](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2304); Commit [`76956e7`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/76956e71cfa3044867213bd65a6f22e96120c108)

**Improvements:**

- Improved support for dual stack connections. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Suggested by Timothée Peignier.*)

  When a host name resolves to more than one address, e.g. IPv4 and IPv6, the next address is tried once the prior address has had reasonable time to connect, following the "happy eyeballs" approach of RFC 8305. A connection attempt that neither fails nor connects no longer uses up all the available time.

  Issue [#2154](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2154); Pull request [#2235](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2235); Commit [`7448fde`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7448fde157c6d22f57b90c0cef11957b0aadf4b3)
- Make meson the primary build system. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  meson is now the build system used for testing and for building the documentation. Functions not used outside their module are now hidden, which makes the binary smaller.

  [`e3d9df3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e3d9df3ae9669ee5749b970d59f8afeb20fd3d9d) Make meson the primary build system.; Pull request [#2264](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2264)

  [`f287178`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f287178b705cc90aa887d66c932e2a223029649b) Set function/variable visibility to hidden.; Pull request [#2265](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2265)
- Detect files that have not changed during non-delta incremental backup. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  During a non-delta diff or incr backup, a file that fits in a single buffer, or any block incremental file, is compared to the file in the prior backup and referenced instead of stored when it has not changed.

  Pull request [#2257](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2257); Commit [`9d91d1b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9d91d1b2f817b543fc8d970af56d1442c325ed7c)
- Prevent invalid recovery when `backup_label` removed. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  When `backup_label` is removed from a restored cluster, PostgreSQL uses the checkpoint in `pg_control` for crash recovery, which nearly always corrupts the cluster. Restore now invalidates the checkpoint in `pg_control`, so recovery fails instead. `pg_controldata` shows the checkpoint as `0/DEAD`.

  Pull request [#2232](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2232); Commit [`e634fd8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e634fd85cedc1ae3aef7c4fa2556d7d73d05cb8a)
- Improve `archive-push` WAL segment queue handling. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  The size of every WAL segment in the queue was read to measure the queue. The size of the first segment is now used for all of them.

  Pull request [#2277](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2277); Commit [`cf17515`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cf17515e409440eecfb04bf7186790d8baa8ab12)
- Limit resume functionality to full backups. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost, Stefan Fercot.*)

  Resume did not work correctly for delta diff and incr backups, and a file that changed since the prior backup is likely to change again before a resume. Only full backups are resumed now.

  Pull request [#2268](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2268); Commit [`dddcbcd`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/dddcbcd8e9a1cae578b0cc5bb2d1aa768330f3c5)
- Update resume functionality for block incremental. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  Resume removed block incremental files, so it worked but copied all of them again. Block incremental files are now kept on resume.

  Pull request [#2296](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2296); Commit [`ff47450`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ff47450402fde5ebc64a08d31791551bee52200e)
- Allow `--version` and `--help` for version and help. (*Reviewed by Greg Sabino Mullane. Suggested by Greg Sabino Mullane.*)

  `--version` and `--help` now work as they do for most programs. Use `help` for help on a specific command or option.

  Pull request [#2263](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2263); Commit [`68db307`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/68db3075d7ac4a48377967ad54e45b95b9ee6c61)
- Add detailed backtrace to autoconf/make build. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  Error stack traces in the autoconf/make build now include detail from libbacktrace, which performance tests showed has no noticeable cost.

  Pull request [#1970](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1970); Commit [`676700d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/676700d8ca6f2e0e0ab8123aa9606c8ec7aa81ae)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Update references to `recovery.conf`. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Suggested by Stephen Frost.*)

  References to `recovery.conf` now include `postgresql.auto.conf`, which newer versions of PostgreSQL use.

  Pull request [#2281](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2281); Commit [`6835178`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/68351785f6816a23526900db595557cf6e3a0056)

### v2.50 Release Notes

*Performance Improvements and Bug Fixes*

*Released January 22, 2024*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix short read in block incremental restore. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Tested by Brent Graveland. Reported by Adol Rodriguez, Brent Graveland.*)

  A compressed super block can end with bytes that are not needed to decompress it but mark the end of the data. Depending on how a buffer lined up with the data, these bytes were not read and the restore failed.

  Issue [#2215](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2215); Issue [#2238](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2238); Pull request [#2256](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2256); Commit [`f4a4af2`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f4a4af299bee93ab7dde599ccdb35becc1c2afad)
- Fix overflow suppressing backup progress in `info` output. (*Fixed by Robert Donovan. Reviewed by Joe Wildish.*)

  The sizes in the lock file were read as 32-bit values, so the `info` command did not report progress for a backup larger than 4GiB.

  Issue [#2251](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2251); Pull request [#2252](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2252); Commit [`25f1489`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/25f14898babf4ad78baef3e8343933a863633975)

**Improvements:**

- Preserve partial files during block incremental delta restore. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  A file smaller than expected was not kept for a block incremental delta restore, although its blocks can be reused, e.g. when retrying a restore that failed or restoring over a smaller file.

  Pull request [#2254](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2254); Commit [`a42614e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a42614e8f3a9e6871c16331f01c2cd765ab181aa)
- Add support for alternate compile-time page sizes. (*Contributed by Viktor Kurilko. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  PostgreSQL can be built with a different page size, e.g. 4096, and forks such as Greenplum do this.

  Pull request [#2197](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2197); Commit [`89d5278`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/89d5278b74ab0d880345575d7ac4c239cd1fce54)
- Skip files truncated during backup when bundling. (*Contributed by Georgy Shelkovy. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  A file truncated to zero after the files were assigned to bundles was still stored in its bundle, and a debug build failed an assertion. These files are now skipped.

  [`02eea55`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/02eea555c7169e99892fdbc8ce7cc1ee59f50509) Skip files truncated during backup when bundling.; Pull request [#2227](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2227)

  [`c879509`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c8795094d418802a0e24d4d19da66973c71b00e5) Allow const checksum buffers to be returned from backupFile().

  [`9049fec`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9049fec2c02c4110e16ffdff7b56f83d8ff32b4a) Refactor skip files truncated during backup when bundling.

  Development in [v2.49](#v249-release-notes):

  [`337da35`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/337da35ab278e058ec4cdffeb4319f1a1e2cb0df) Add test to show behavior of bundled files truncated during backup.
- Improve SFTP storage error messages. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Pull request [#2249](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2249); Commit [`7c17eec`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7c17eec3db455d3e8e660f4f8247b6975517648f)

### v2.49 Release Notes

*Remove PostgreSQL 9.3 Support*

*Released November 27, 2023*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix regression in retries. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Reported by Norman Adkins, Tanel Suurhans, Jordan English, Timothée Peignier.*)

  v2.48 dropped the retry that was made when an operation used up its whole timeout, so a timeout became an error, e.g. waiting for a socket to be writable on an object store. Two retries are now made even when the timeout has expired.

  [`3c116e1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3c116e1829a0df62aaf50ce241c63d829eb438e0) Remove unused header.

  [`fa5b2d4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/fa5b2d44ad9f7591517eecaaaaeb07e9500dd51c) Fix regression in retries.; Issue [#2200](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2200); Issue [#2214](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2214); Pull request [#2221](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2221)
- Fix recursive path remove in SFTP storage driver. (*Fixed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Reported by Luc.*)

  Some SFTP servers report permission denied instead of a failure when a directory is unlinked as a file, so removing a path recursively failed on those servers.

  Issue [#2219](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2219); Pull request [#2226](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2226); Commit [`c4dc466`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c4dc4665f88e9d67a3b66f44cd59d6dd4bb5107f)

**Improvements:**

- Remove support for PostgreSQL `9.3`. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  PostgreSQL 9.3 is no longer supported, per the policy of supporting five end-of-life versions of PostgreSQL.

  Pull request [#2220](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2220); Commit [`dcf0781`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/dcf078198761a2cfe9be6b4c7f4b4fb07ef53cbf)

**Documentation Features:**

- Document maintainer options. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  Maintainer options are now documented in their own section with the caveats for using them, and `pg-version-force` is no longer internal.

  Pull request [#2202](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2202); Commit [`21c8c8a`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/21c8c8a66cb06eba714bfa41bed7239af3d14f08)
- Update point-in-time recovery documentation for PostgreSQL >= 13.

  Since PostgreSQL 13 recovery errors when the recovery target is not reached. The point-in-time recovery section now works for PostgreSQL 13 and later as well as for earlier versions.

  Commit [`eb69e2e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/eb69e2ee6339047f0145b751437ac2bec82bb5ab)

**Test Suite Improvements:**

- Allow `config/load` unit test to run without `libssh2` installed. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele. Suggested by Wu Ning.*)

  Issue [#2193](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2193); Pull request [#2194](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2194); Commit [`e2b734e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e2b734eff97e3fac32895b799cc32126013aae27)

### v2.48 Release Notes

*Repository Storage Tags*

*Released September 25, 2023*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix issue restoring block incremental without a block list. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Tested by Burak Yurdakul. Reported by Burak Yurdakul.*)

  A block map with no blocks, which is written when a file timestamp changes but the file does not, was not decrypted when it followed a file stored without block incremental in the same bundle read. With enough affected files the retries were exhausted and the restore failed.

  Issue [#2182](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2182); Pull request [#2184](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2184); Commit [`31de127`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/31de127cf42438b1fac04c161c47264aa1fb1060)

**Features:**

- Add `--repo-storage-tag` option to create object tags. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost, Stefan Fercot, Timothée Peignier.*)

  Set `repo-storage-tag` to add tags to the objects written to an S3, GCS, or Azure repository. Tags are set for each repository.

  Issue [#2150](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2150); Pull request [#2159](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2159); Commit [`1b4e0cc`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1b4e0cce5f3ff5ea3ee0b889e6d4ce4de1430b04)
- Add known hosts checking for SFTP storage driver. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by Stephen Frost, David Steele.*)

  SFTP host keys are now checked against the known hosts files by default, i.e. `repo-sftp-host-key-check-type=strict`. Set `repo-sftp-host-key-check-type=accept-new` to add new hosts automatically, or `repo-sftp-host-key-check-type=none` to skip the check as before.

  Pull request [#2125](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2125); Commit [`ce9ba0f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ce9ba0fadee652d409488bee701ea6a51cafec75)
- Support for dual stack connections. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  Each address a host name resolves to is now tried in turn, e.g. an IPv6 address with no route to the host and then an IPv4 address, until one connects or the timeout expires. Repeated retry errors are also summarized in error messages.

  [`5314dbf`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/5314dbffc761bc7507db02693f2421c1578efe10) Adjust Wait object to be more accurate when nested.

  [`c180513`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c1805134b370f0c5c54c40a2df8978599328784f) Aggregate error retries in ErrorRetry output.

  [`edbd520`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/edbd520c81f5d437963aaf42d1c79b2c51d01665) Refactor address list lookup to include all returned addresses.; Pull request [#2174](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2174)

  [`39bb8a0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/39bb8a0d3a96eef63219dff262a9306a4f59dae9) Support for dual stack connections.; Issue [#2154](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2154); Pull request [#2175](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2175)
- Add backup size completed/total to `info` command JSON output. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The JSON output of the `info` command now includes the completed and total size of a backup in progress, so progress can be monitored by other programs.

  Issue [#2007](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2007); Pull request [#2141](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2141); Commit [`e3fcd8c`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e3fcd8cd9e0be42a32fc4cbd2ae3db2c072eabcd)

**Improvements:**

- Multi-stanza check command. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  When `stanza` is not specified, the `check` command now checks every stanza in the configuration.

  Pull request [#2057](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2057); Commit [`1141dc2`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1141dc20708cf0c68296f83e74c872a7db9c2bd9)
- Retry reads of `pg_control` until checksum is valid. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot, Stephen Frost.*)

  On some filesystems, e.g. ext4, a read of `pg_control` during a concurrent write can return torn data. The read is now retried until the checksum is valid.

  Pull request [#2131](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2131); Commit [`f42d927`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f42d927d2dcd58cf5a80d68eaf6888bcf2c67a4b)
- Optimize WAL segment check after successful backup. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  The check for WAL segments after a backup listed the storage once for each segment. Each WAL directory is now listed once and the list is reused.

  Pull request [#2169](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2169); Commit [`9d3a605`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9d3a605900644059ff0e030d71a818b3965eb031)
- Improve GCS multi-part performance. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson.*)

  Chunks of a multi-part upload are now uploaded as soon as they are available.

  Pull request [#2162](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2162); Commit [`6cb9c40`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6cb9c40fb8ea18e6572bf3f6e44bbb1d8f922682)
- Allow `archive-get` command to run when stanza is stopped. (*Reviewed by Tom Swartz, David Christensen, Reid Thompson.*)

  The `archive-get` command does not write to the repository, so it can now run while the stanza is stopped, as `restore` can.

  Commit [`fbd992a`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/fbd992adc7b18db5e70774d675c160c8c8e2f031)
- Accept leading tilde in paths for SFTP public/private keys. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Paths for `repo-sftp-private-key-file` and `repo-sftp-public-key-file` may now begin with `~`, as documented.

  Pull request [#2136](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2136); Commit [`eb32d6d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/eb32d6de5e1575a37efb166abbf7df400d93352e)
- Reload GCS credentials before renewing authentication token. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Suggested by Daniel Farina.*)

  The service key file is now read again before the authentication token is renewed, so the key can be replaced while a command is running.

  Pull request [#2072](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2072); Commit [`995a8e9`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/995a8e96691d23eaf3914a425669f89deb3ce002)

**Documentation Bug Fixes:**

- Fix configuration reference example for the `tls-server-address` option. (*Fixed by Hartmut Goebel. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The example `::*` was not valid. Use `*` for IPv4 or `::` for IPv6.

  Pull request [#2165](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2165); Commit [`43524a4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/43524a47a4c6a847f76514812f94b87cb7af4c05)
- Fix command reference example for the `filter` option.

  Commit [`cb3ff6e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cb3ff6ed4336af9e4c0643d8224d5bc3d790a655)

**Test Suite Improvements:**

- Allow `storage/sftp` unit test to run without `libssh2` installed. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele. Suggested by Wu Ning.*)

  Issue [#2163](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2163); Pull request [#2177](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2177); Commit [`c3c0834`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c3c0834e170196a1241a368779162fcca65f9ad5)

### v2.47 Release Notes

*Performance Improvements and Bug Fixes*

*Released July 24, 2023*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Preserve block incremental info in manifest during delta backup. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Reported by Francisco Miguel Biete Banon.*)

  When a file changed so that it would not use block incremental if it were new, e.g. it shrank below the block incremental limit or its timestamp moved far enough into the past, its block incremental info was lost from the manifest of a delta backup even though the file could still be referenced to the prior backup.

  [`1d42aed`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1d42aed152563fff6ec70c517d86eb39158b3f20) Improve validation of referenced files in command/backup unit test.

  [`4c27d74`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/4c27d74bbd3a44d8ab1af140277d3cd8302060c2) Preserve block incremental info in manifest during delta backup.; Issue [#2112](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2112); Pull request [#2123](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2123)
- Fix block incremental file names in `verify` command. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson. Reported by Francisco Miguel Biete Banon.*)

  The `verify` command used the compression extension instead of `.pgbi` for block incremental files stored outside a bundle, so it could not find them.

  Issue [#2112](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2112); Pull request [#2120](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2120); Commit [`cdb7e26`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cdb7e26350822b55c00771d6d575242b1202e741)
- Fix spurious automatic delta backup on backup from standby. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Reported by krmozejko, Don Seiler.*)

  File sizes on the standby can differ from the primary, e.g. for free space maps, so the next incremental backup could see a size change on the primary and switch to a checksum delta, which can take much longer. The original size is now kept in the manifest and used for the comparison.

  Issue [#2020](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2020); Pull request [#2124](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2124); Commit [`5ed6f8d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/5ed6f8df1487174985c42d6e13c367d70c2d8b8a)
- Skip `recovery.signal` for PostgreSQL >= 12 when recovery `type=none`. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Reported by T.Anastacio.*)

  With `type=none`, `recovery.signal` is no longer written for PostgreSQL >= 12, which matches the behavior for earlier versions.

  Issue [#2062](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2062); Pull request [#2070](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2070); Commit [`9cceed6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9cceed6ac4a32c14a797eba89a4ad1dc06d89d80)
- Fix unique label generation for diff/incr backup. (*Fixed by Andrey Sokolov. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  With at least two full backups and the latest expired, a diff or incr backup failed as if the clock had gone backward, because its label was compared with the label of the expired full backup.

  Issue [#1873](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1873); Pull request [#2104](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2104); Commit [`0ac0934`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/0ac09344dc17fb54e250705ce49399b86329bb3d)
- Fix time-based archive expiration when no backups are expired. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  When time-based retention was met but no backups were expired, archive was not expired and "time-based archive retention not met" was logged. One more backup was then needed to expire the earlier WAL.

  Issue [#2103](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2103); Pull request [#2109](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2109); Commit [`762498f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/762498f4cd5f1425db4c176fc707926d62cc7c04)

**Improvements:**

- Improve performance of SFTP storage driver. (*Contributed by Stephen Frost, Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The SFTP storage driver waited for a fixed time when libssh2 could not continue. It now waits until the socket can be read or written.

  [`e280ed9`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e280ed9098a6f5da9bdabb541e9c69d4f24ffcf2) Make result code handling in storage/sftp more consistent.

  [`125676a`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/125676ae0e98d3730d2dc0d47e37ca1df145d044) Cleanup of init/handshake in storageSftpNew().

  [`28b6b2d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/28b6b2d4659d78ce67c1bd3104d96e5705d955c9) Improve performance of SFTP storage driver.; Pull request [#2089](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2089)
- Add timezone offset to `info` command date/time output. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot, Philip Hurst. Suggested by Philip Hurst.*)

  Timestamps in the `info` command output now include the timezone offset, so they can be used directly as a recovery target, e.g. with `--target`.

  [`9efd5cd`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9efd5cd0bb40c5dc2fcac2e21c9b62e554b94db6) Add timezone offset to info command date/time output.; Issue [#2093](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2093); Pull request [#2095](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2095)

  [`aa229a1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/aa229a1deef3afeebb66db1443b19e24559adf96) Modify time_t casts based on complaints from Coverity.
- Centralize error handling for unsupported features. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  Errors for features that were not compiled in, e.g. lz4, are now more detailed and consistent. Specifying SFTP storage when it was not compiled in no longer fails an assertion.

  [`de46276`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/de46276bf60b8a2181621b123d1a99e56808a089) Refactor allow list processing in config/parse module.

  [`36ff81d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/36ff81dc6ff7e98e40c0c05a4a343a71b91b1678) Centralize error handling for unsupported features.; Pull request [#2075](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2075)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Clarify preference to install from packages in the user guide. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Suggested by dr-kd.*)

  Pull request [#2090](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2090); Commit [`a36f480`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a36f480c85c7add6fdf0e5517748d740e3a7b424)

### v2.46 Release Notes

*Block Incremental Backup and SFTP Storage*

*Released May 22, 2023*

**Features:**

- Block incremental backup. (*Reviewed by John Morris, Stephen Frost, Stefan Fercot.*)

  Block incremental stores only the parts of a file that changed since the prior backup, which saves space and time in diff and incr backups of large files. Enable it with `repo-block`, which requires `repo-bundle`.

  [`dd4e526`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/dd4e52679ecdc43539bd038b5aa9bd4ff9055cbf) Removing chunking and block numbers from incremental block list.; Pull request [#2029](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2029)

  [`822f2a5`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/822f2a584260a012b833455a7a2bd1c8487e3010) Finalize block incremental feature.; Pull request [#2053](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2053)
- SFTP support for repository storage. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by Stephen Frost, David Steele.*)

  Set `repo-type=sftp` and `repo-sftp-host` to store the repository on an SFTP server.

  Pull request [#1959](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1959); Commit [`87087fa`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/87087fac231d2110160b3219daee3f9fb91aec02)

  Development in [v2.42](#v242-release-notes):

  [`01b81f9`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/01b81f9d374701eb489dd1888bcba25fb5d6df84) Move link creation to storage interface.; Pull request [#1879](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1879)
- PostgreSQL 16 support. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  Adds the catalog version and WAL magic number for PostgreSQL 16.

  Pull request [#2051](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2051); Commit [`3fc3690`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3fc3690dd77ef5fa032abb006558184164db53ec)

**Improvements:**

- Allow page header checks to be skipped. (*Reviewed by David Christensen. Suggested by David Christensen.*)

  The page header checks made while verifying page checksums can report false errors for encrypted pages. Set `page-header-check=n` to skip them. They are enabled by default.

  Pull request [#2045](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2045); Commit [`f5e6bc2`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f5e6bc26983396ec51c6579aade37db39807f3a5)
- Avoid `chown()` on recovery files during restore. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot, Marcelo Henrique Neppel. Suggested by Marcelo Henrique Neppel.*)

  Restore skipped `chown()` for restored files that already had the right owner but not for the recovery files it writes. The Posix driver now also skips `chown()` when the owner is already correct.

  Issue [#2036](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2036); Pull request [#2038](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2038); Commit [`3ff88ff`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3ff88ffbb495fd669e9a361478e60192930ee0a1)
- Add error retry detail for HTTP retries.

  An error after HTTP retries now reports how many retries were made and for how long.

  [`801e396`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/801e396dace2a4d9f4125ef0e751c90dc0cc644a) Move error modules to common/error directory.

  [`df419b3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/df419b34b55a15a902ff5cc1912aaebc7e023738) Improve retry error messages.

  [`f33e1cf`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f33e1cfb1600643820a1ae76bdeb15c62ac1fcc7) Add error retry detail for HTTP retries.

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Add warning about using recovery `type=none`. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  Recovery `type=none` does not increment the timeline at the end of recovery, which can cause PostgreSQL to archive duplicate WAL that is rejected and fills the disk, and can make tools like `pg_rewind` fail or corrupt the cluster. The documentation now warns against it.

  Issue [#2062](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2062); Pull request [#2069](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2069); Commit [`a28b72c`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a28b72c62431bcf9df02791219d9d6f5d0e60b54)
- Add note about running `stanza-create` on already-created repositories.

  Stanzas that already exist are skipped, so `stanza-create` is safe to run again, e.g. after a new repository is configured.

  Commit [`0c1f823`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/0c1f823e7a3abf1cadb44de3832ec1ddb2da7ec8)

### v2.45 Release Notes

*Block Incremental Backup (BETA)*

*Released March 20, 2023*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Skip writing `recovery.signal` by default for restores of offline backups. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Reported by Marcel Borger.*)

  On PostgreSQL >= 12, `recovery.signal` makes PostgreSQL fail to start when the backup was made offline with `wal_level=minimal`. Restore now skips writing it for offline backups unless `type` is set explicitly, e.g. for Point-in-Time Recovery from an offline backup made with a higher `wal_level`.

  [`0818601`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/0818601c055724bde3b97d4b02d3a38b0a53899a) Set online flag in manifest in command/restore unit tests.

  [`2fa7e53`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/2fa7e53c5d4b5a5fd79f62d39763746727a5b9be) Skip writing recovery.signal by default for restores of offline backups.; Issue [#2018](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/2018); Pull request [#2019](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2019)

**Features:**

- Block incremental backup (BETA). (*Reviewed by John Morris, Stephen Frost, Stefan Fercot.*)

  Block incremental stores only the parts of a file that changed since the prior backup instead of the entire file. Enable it with `repo-block`, which requires `repo-bundle`. The feature is in beta and also requires the `beta` option. Do not use it in production: the on-disk format will change before the GA release.

  [`089fae0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/089fae035bf0a9778fff0527ea3cbb113625a403) Add block incremental to real/all test output.

  [`3feed38`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3feed389a2199454db68e446851323498b45db20) Improve IoChunkedRead end-of-file handling.

  [`d520816`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/d520816acf9ecb01ef805cecbc9de7e8d0a9f468) Remove parameter list from deltaMapNew().

  [`779efe0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/779efe0d7a54340141e01597279e9ab1d0bd0773) Consistently declare block incremental size as size_t.

  [`dffc933`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/dffc933384e4327e2fd52868dd19668ed9d3ed91) Rename DeltaMap to BlockHash.

  [`a9867cb`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a9867cb0b8e8d7abcac9b18b76e3e490c265f3cb) Add repo-block-age-map and repo-block-size-map options.

  [`16ac5ee`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/16ac5ee8d3b926464cd27e8f064579f1e7935f4c) Rename block incremental manifest keys.

  [`da91858`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/da9185870222057b97365f5db0acf797f959ec5e) Add optional raw format for compression types.

  [`7e5adc0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7e5adc03594a3b7a975001e80902a51df3ddd577) Use raw compression/encryption to bundling and block incremental backup.

  [`8b5153a`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/8b5153ad210011812c8fe6db2f2ed829a5aa543a) Block-level incremental backup super blocks.; Pull request [#1996](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1996)

  [`210bed4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/210bed4511ae5523b170eb193b877346d787140d) Use xxHash instead of SHA-1 for block incremental checksums.

  [`6252c0e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6252c0e4485caee362edec13302a5f735a69bff4) Exclude backup set size from info for block incremental backups.

  [`1119a53`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1119a5353911d6d326edac325f530bb796a806ae) Rename BlockHash to BlockChecksum.

  [`6b409d0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6b409d049eb935146f7996b75b97744b75c411a9) Update default block size and super block values based on testing.

  [`1281a6e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1281a6eaf8552232c8c9af4d7fc15c082a7794d9) Ensure no continuations when block size equals super block size.

  [`5c1f78d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/5c1f78d4dd62c37218285d13b09a9450eb3a588e) Fix typo in blockIncrProcess().

  [`c30d3e4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c30d3e439b77d544477e289606d30d0c8ffe301f) Block incremental map fixes and improvements.; Pull request [#2026](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2026)

  [`24f7252`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/24f725212d0c1dedc0f34fe4e939e481585ff697) Add beta feature infrastructure.

  [`04d224b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/04d224b88e73194ff1c6497dbecaaff392008dfe) Add beta feature and documentation for block incremental backup.

  Development in [v2.44](#v244-release-notes):

  [`912eec6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/912eec63bb971c10a545e582f628c982f4e00005) Block-level incremental backup.; Issue [#959](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/959); Issue [#1806](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1806); Issue [#1913](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1913); Pull request [#1916](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1916)

  Development in [v2.42](#v242-release-notes):

  [`1ea6a41`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1ea6a4142e47e2e0b56911ad1f028dfcddc5fd64) Improve manifest file updates.; Pull request [#1888](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1888)

  [`c647bcb`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c647bcb50954f623d29104cdb8a1457ed8e94579) Add manifest flags for file processing during backup.; Pull request [#1889](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1889)

  [`102ce5d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/102ce5dee47e8d9a8d2eeaf2b2cc2bac105914ef) Add persistent reference list to manifest.; Pull request [#1890](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1890)

  [`ac99201`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ac99201c0ebde616279214e600e49fdf506f7469) Add bufferSize to cvtUInt64FromVarInt128() to further limit reads.

  [`5602f17`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/5602f179a177a8513b96c762590829c012c28175) Add varint-128 encode/decode to IoRead/IoWrite.; Pull request [#1891](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1891)

  [`352cbf1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/352cbf1d5738a720292b5067051a05efc2b9a8ac) Update ManifestFile booleans to bit fields.

  [`e7e106f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e7e106f781be01027d4829b258902549eb36eb7b) Use read-only storage to calculate checksum in restoreFile().

  [`909be41`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/909be412f8ad0d87f23ce2a2dbca94c3e0732f9f) Swap command/backup and command/restore unit tests.

  [`1730ef4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1730ef4ac3d88137bd218e6128b0107a8bf2c121) Add noTruncate flag to storageNewWriteP().; Pull request [#1898](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1898)

  [`fee38c2`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/fee38c2c7cc3ae318bf0d3b50fe23c22900c2311) Pass filters to remote storage as a handler array.

  [`5fceee8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/5fceee88a9439460bf24082f275ec2e695cc6bf6) Add backupFileRepoPathP().

  [`221db61`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/221db610d2a054ed00ad27377663941abfb9fb98) Shorten names in real/all integration test matrix.

  [`c9db7bc`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c9db7bc274211820030c4ab6ab8c70c1b1896254) Update cipherBlockNew() to allow optional parameters.

  [`58b3c91`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/58b3c91babc6af9e003e5ef09bf99becf8a6f827) Add raw mode to CipherBlock to save space.

**Improvements:**

- Keep only one all-default group index. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  An option that does not apply, e.g. `repo2-s3-key` when `repo2-type` is not `s3`, could add a phantom repository that has only default values. Repository and PostgreSQL indexes with only default values are now removed unless no other index remains.

  Pull request [#2011](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/2011); Commit [`1648c13`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1648c133d6bc1ffd0682ae6219934d00295aa580)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Add explicit instructions for upgrading between `2.x` versions. (*Contributed by Christophe Courtois. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  A new user guide section explains that upgrading between `2.x` versions requires only installing the new binaries. Downgrading is also possible when no features unsupported by the older version were used.

  Issue [#1774](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1774); Pull request [#1993](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1993); Commit [`15d5dcd`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/15d5dcdd3bb387aab28bbe7262011c38c7d6d22c)
- Remove references to SSH made obsolete when TLS was introduced.

  The help for `repo-host`, `pg-host`, and their port options no longer refers to SSH as the only remote protocol. Details about SSH compression were also removed.

  Commit [`c5907a2`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c5907a2e7193ca8f79cf56dca23e0df118e686c3)

### v2.44 Release Notes

*Remove PostgreSQL 9.0/9.1/9.2 Support*

*Released January 30, 2023*

**Improvements:**

- Remove support for PostgreSQL `9.0`/`9.1`/`9.2`. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  pgBackRest now supports ten versions of PostgreSQL: the five supported versions and the last five EOL versions. As of PostgreSQL 15 that excludes `9.0`, `9.1`, and `9.2`.

  Pull request [#1941](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1941); Commit [`77c721e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/77c721eb635c6d757d2d5ed6f2ffc083720c10a7)
- Restore errors when no backup matches the current version of PostgreSQL. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele. Suggested by Soulou.*)

  After a `stanza-upgrade` with no new backup, restore used the latest backup, which was made from the prior PostgreSQL version. Replicas built this way could fail. Restore now errors in this case unless `set` or `type=time`/`type=lsn` is specified.

  Issue [#1942](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1942); Pull request [#1967](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1967); Commit [`b9be4fa`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b9be4fa54039e2c79fad0dc1552c7ffaf883004c)
- Add `compress-level` range checking for each `compress-type`. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Suggested by gkleen, ViperRu.*)

  The `compress-level` range was `0-9` for every `compress-type`. This is the range for `gz` but a subset of what `lz4` and `zst` allow. Each type now has its own range, e.g. `-7` to `22` for `zst`.

  Issue [#1216](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1216); Issue [#1931](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1931); Pull request [#1962](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1962); Commit [`44da314`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/44da314adb2b63f96b1d571f822e91a0e412e984)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Add warning about enabling "hierarchical namespace" on Azure storage. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Suggested by Vojtech Galda, Pluggi, asjonos.*)

  With "hierarchical namespace" enabled, Azure requires directories to be deleted separately and `expire` fails. The user guide now warns not to enable it.

  Issue [#1770](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1770); Pull request [#1980](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1980); Commit [`3aea997`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3aea997df5ace85251d0579ccbe13fa938f587ee)
- Add replacement for linefeeds in monitoring example. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Suggested by rudonx, gmustdie, Ivan Shelestov.*)

  The monitoring example failed when `info` output contained a linefeed, e.g. in some error messages, because `COPY` converts `\n` to a linefeed that `jsonb` rejects. The example now replaces linefeeds before converting to `jsonb`.

  Issue [#1910](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1910); Pull request [#1963](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1963); Commit [`ae258f6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ae258f604e634f1d2a760ffdb92dd359fd1129bb)
- Clarify `target-action` behavior on various PostgreSQL versions. (*Contributed by Chris Bandy. Reviewed by David Steele, Anton Kurochkin, Stefan Fercot. Suggested by Anton Kurochkin, Chris Bandy.*)

  The effect of `pause` depends on `hot_standby` and the PostgreSQL version. With `hot_standby=off`, `pause` acts like `shutdown` on PostgreSQL >= 12 and like `promote` on earlier versions.

  Issue [#987](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/987); Issue [#1314](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1314); Pull request [#1960](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1960); Commit [`84a3ff8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/84a3ff8b7ac1243c0b47c6585697f4fcf3c12065)
- Updates and clarifications to index page. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  The section about other backup solutions not supporting parallel processing was no longer accurate and has been reworded. Other sections were updated for current terminology and clarity.

  Pull request [#1966](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1966); Commit [`36ee30d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/36ee30d118ce6cc7720653a3d3a49a06c655c463)
- Add dark mode to the website. (*Suggested by Stephen Frost.*)

  The website uses dark colors when the browser prefers a dark color scheme.

  Commit [`ed818b3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ed818b31867c640d72ef829dd36cd0a7feff12df)

### v2.43 Release Notes

*Bug Fix*

*Released November 28, 2022*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix missing reference in diff/incr backup. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Reported by Marcel Borger, ulfedf, jaymefSO.*)

  In v2.42 a diff or incr backup failed with "unable to find '...' in string list" when its prior backup was made by an earlier version. The reference list built for a manifest from an earlier version now includes the backup's own label.

  [`28f6604`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/28f660482aa5abb97850a9d7c89c2e5e9f23a4c9) Move manifestBackupLabelSet() test in info/manifest unit.

  [`c4bf775`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c4bf775099c85e3cd081f9c1ce35efd4c71d9161) Fix missing reference in diff/incr backup.; Issue [#1933](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1933); Pull request [#1935](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1935)

**Improvements:**

- Add hint when an option is specified without an index. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  An option that requires an index, e.g. `repo-storage-ca-file`, now errors with a hint that shows the indexed name, e.g. `repo1-storage-ca-file`. A deprecated option also gets a hint with its current name.

  Issue [#1925](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1925); Pull request [#1929](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1929); Commit [`3f363cb`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3f363cb3aed1a7c6836ee6c32eae600129a6f6b8)

### v2.42 Release Notes

*Bug Fixes*

*Released November 22, 2022*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix memory leak in file bundle `backup`/`restore`. (*Reviewed by John Morris. Tested by Oscar. Reported by Oscar.*)

  Restore freed some I/O objects for bundled files only after every file in the bundle was restored. With many small files in a bundle, memory use grew until restore failed with "unable to allocate". Backup had a similar, smaller leak when resuming.

  Issue [#1877](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1877); Pull request [#1878](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1878); Commit [`cd8db7d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cd8db7d9e53e041ad6fb31f2f015541c1d24abc2)
- Fix protocol error on short read of remote file. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  A remote file read that stopped early or failed partway left the protocol in a bad state. The next command failed with "client state is 'data-get' but expected 'idle'" and the original error was lost. The rest of the file is now read when the file is closed.

  [`ea162e8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ea162e821634919b7c839e866b99bf82010dbacc) Move storageReadRemoteOpen() in storage/remote/read module.

  [`7967c75`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7967c750d83466d030081e9deb1d70a1ba240271) Fix protocol error on short read of remote file.; Pull request [#1900](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1900)

**Improvements:**

- Do not store references for zero-length files when bundling. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  Bundled diff/incr backups stored a reference to a prior backup for zero-length files, which are not stored in any backup. The reference was ignored on restore but used space in the manifest.

  Pull request [#1887](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1887); Commit [`f981fb4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f981fb45d9931e597afec764e13b1a53d8365d25)
- Use more generic descriptions for `pg_start_backup()`/`pg_stop_backup()`. (*Reviewed by Greg Sabino Mullane, David Christensen. Suggested by Greg Sabino Mullane.*)

  PostgreSQL 15 renamed these functions to `pg_backup_start()`/`pg_backup_stop()`. The logs and documentation now use descriptions that do not depend on the PostgreSQL version.

  Pull request [#1901](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1901); Commit [`ddd966c`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ddd966cadc30440a81100144204d4084a0f2ac3a)

**Test Suite Improvements:**

- Update `test.pl` `--psql-bin` option to match command-line help. (*Contributed by Koshi Shibagaki. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The help showed `--psql-bin` but the option was named `--pgsql-bin`. The option now has the name shown in the help.

  Pull request [#1920](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1920); Commit [`3ad5884`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3ad588443b452e04f3abcc107e825dc47e078103)

### v2.41 Release Notes

*Backup Annotations*

*Released September 19, 2022*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix incorrect time expiration being used for non-default repositories. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Reported by Adam Brusselback.*)

  When `expire` ran without `repo`, the time-based `repo-retention-full` for every repository was read from the lowest-numbered repository. Each repository now uses its own value.

  Issue [#1852](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1852); Pull request [#1854](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1854); Commit [`eda7f81`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/eda7f81ee4fd6818ce458768144fc3fc1e1f0f21)
- Fix issue when listing directories recursively with a filter. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Reported by Efremov Egor.*)

  `repo-ls` with `recurse` and `filter` could fail with a double free or segfault when the last entry in a directory was a directory that had entries. A parent list was freed before the entries of its subdirectories were processed.

  Issue [#1870](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1870); Pull request [#1871](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1871); Commit [`c39c9f2`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c39c9f220ed328b4175d912e109697b726e9ea9b)

**Features:**

- Backup key/value annotations. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele. Suggested by Adam Berlin.*)

  Add key/value annotations to a backup with the `annotation` option of the `backup` command, and add, modify, or remove them later with the new `annotate` command. The `info` command shows annotations in text output when `set` is specified and always includes them in JSON output.

  Issue [#1709](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1709); Pull request [#1724](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1724); Commit [`381fd0a`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/381fd0a5a4de61624615b150ba98beeafac31f7e)

**Improvements:**

- Support `--set` in JSON output for `info` command. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele. Suggested by Anton Kurochkin.*)

  With `set`, JSON output from `info` now includes the details that text output already showed, e.g. the error and tablespace lists.

  Issue [#1484](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1484); Pull request [#1501](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1501); Commit [`db75ffd`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/db75ffd27037d690a11eb02902f929250fe2bf5b)
- Allow upload chunk size to be configured for object stores. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Suggested by Anton Glushakov.*)

  Set `repo-storage-upload-chunk-size` to change the size of each upload chunk for S3, GCS, or Azure. Larger chunks can improve performance but use more memory. The defaults are unchanged.

  [`ff1188f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ff1188f92dfc9e8f6d4aeca8b71a9886f24f58b4) Allow quote tag in command-line help.

  [`b05d31f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b05d31f531b55b747175cf94c963f95574c54d2b) Allow upload chunk size to be configured for object stores.; Issue [#1843](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1843); Pull request [#1847](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1847)
- Update archive.info timestamps after a successful backup. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Suggested by Alex Richman.*)

  `archive.info` and its copy were written only by `stanza-create` and `stanza-upgrade`, and an object store lifecycle policy that expires old files could remove them. Their timestamps are now updated after each successful backup.

  Issue [#1853](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1853); Pull request [#1855](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1855); Commit [`3b5df1e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3b5df1e089234d425dd225f8c1976755dc947c8a)
- Move standby timeline check after checkpoint. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Tested by Keith Fiske. Suggested by Keith Fiske.*)

  The check that the standby is on the expected timeline used `pg_control` as it was before the backup started. Right after a promotion the standby may not have written the new timeline to `pg_control` yet, and the backup failed with "standby is on timeline ... but expected ...". The check now runs after the checkpoint.

  Issue [#1816](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1816); Pull request [#1819](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1819); Commit [`c267ba5`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c267ba51b1778b5872f4ee928a9614545d743f3f)
- Improve warning message on backup resume. (*Suggested by Cynthia Shang.*)

  The warning read as if the user needed to take action. It now states that invalid files will be removed and then the backup will resume.

  Commit [`cbbe93f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cbbe93f592d8097393c5ffe214f0a8c5fb0549c1)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Add absolute path for `kill` in `pgbackrest.service`. (*Suggested by Don Seiler.*)

  The `pgbackrest.service` example now uses `/bin/kill` in `ExecReload`. Older versions of systemd require an absolute path.

  Commit [`e9ff524`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e9ff5248039eedb25da2fee550ec64fcebbc51c7)

### v2.40 Release Notes

*OpenSSL 3 Support*

*Released July 18, 2022*

**NOTE TO PACKAGERS**: An experimental meson build has been added but packagers should continue to use the autoconf/make build for the foreseeable future.

**Improvements:**

- OpenSSL 3 support. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  pgBackRest now builds with OpenSSL 3. Deprecation warnings are suppressed, and an unexpected EOF is detected with the error codes that OpenSSL 3 reports.

  Pull request [#1767](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1767); Commit [`08242ee`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/08242ee6ac93b9eb9c05cc39e3db15bbafb9052c)
- Create snapshot when listing contents of a path. (*Reviewed by John Morris, Stephen Frost.*)

  A path listed without sorting was read while its entries were processed. When files were deleted during the listing, some filesystems skipped files that still existed and the path could not be removed. Listings now always use a snapshot of the path.

  [`61ca9b5`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/61ca9b58a000aa8789ef3267682519bdb15e8a6b) Replace hrnStorageInfoListCallback() with TEST_STORAGE_LIST() in tests.

  [`1c0bf0b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1c0bf0b15da8c0d15cc639851ff1d1bd05b51d06) Reorder null user/group checks in remote protocol and add tests.

  [`72960bb`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/72960bbf179b4e8051bd4fc50be57d95159e9e13) Rename strTrunc() to strTruncIdx() and add strTrunc().

  [`75623d4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/75623d4583bfdaccb3a19b935fd04d744b363923) Create snapshot when listing contents of a path.; Issue [#1754](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1754); Pull request [#1805](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1805)
- Force `target-timeline=current` when restore `type=immediate`. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  On PostgreSQL >= 12 the default target timeline is `latest`, and recovery could fail when the latest timeline in the repository cannot be reached from the backup. Restore with `type=immediate` does not change timelines, so it now sets the target timeline to `current`.

  Pull request [#1814](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1814); Commit [`364af16`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/364af1635d40431276cc6fd87d6ddd689b611e09)
- Truncate files during delta `restore` when they are larger than expected. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  Delta restore copied a file from the repository whenever its size differed from the manifest. A larger file is now truncated to the size in the manifest and copied only when its checksum does not match. Files that were only appended to, e.g. logs, are no longer copied.

  Pull request [#1758](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1758); Commit [`7ec51e7`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7ec51e7e6279ea576ad3b4b5c2ea1fc389d08ab5)
- Disable incremental manifest save when `resume=n`. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  During a backup the manifest is saved periodically for use by resume. These saves are now skipped when `resume=n`.

  Pull request [#1760](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1760); Commit [`60d70fa`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/60d70fa66fc3f709914f40dd088bac1b467001f2)
- Set backup percent complete to zero before copy start. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The progress of a running backup was not reported until the first file was copied. It is now set to zero when copying starts.

  Pull request [#1759](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1759); Commit [`38ad283`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/38ad2838d553febae1b7fe0244d942dfaa9cf548)
- Use S3 `IsTruncated` flag to determine list continuation. (*Reviewed by John Morris. Tested by Soulou. Suggested by Christian Montagne.*)

  Recent versions of Scality send an empty `NextContinuationToken` when a list is not truncated, which caused an infinite loop. The `IsTruncated` flag now determines whether a list continues, and a missing or empty token for a truncated list is an error.

  Issue [#1748](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1748); Pull request [#1783](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1783); Commit [`845d82f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/845d82f6825d4101e6a465a435ef056ff6ec15a2)

**Documentation Bug Fixes:**

- Skip internal options in the configuration reference. (*Reported by Francisco Miguel Biete Banon.*)

  Options that are internal only for some commands were listed in the configuration reference. The command-line help was not affected.

  Commit [`2643050`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/2643050be0dc4b7363c434f58373919bd6fdac68)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Add link to PostgreSQL configuration in repository host section. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Suggested by Julien Cigar.*)

  Pull request [#1809](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1809); Commit [`74a4ac8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/74a4ac801db921f7b11b63b9f927228c6a6af861)

**Test Suite Improvements:**

- Add experimental Meson build. (*Reviewed by Eli Schwartz, Sam Bassaly.*)

  Meson has simpler syntax and better performance than autoconf/make. It is used for code generation and test builds, and errors on anything but a debug build. Packagers should continue to use autoconf/make.

  [`2feaaea`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/2feaaeaac803d6c0193477b56dbeb622fd991156) Add .inc extension to C files included in other C files.

  [`b8fc20d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b8fc20d5b8e7ca003ff39f29ea6e3ba57653f55c) Add experimental Meson build.; Pull request [#1765](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1765)

  [`665da12`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/665da12ae7f519077512e60ce3a5ec6c6a9997fd) Update meson.build include comment to be more general.

  [`9fd85a1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9fd85a104ad56061b028afd9057ce8c1d636d3dd) Disable meson for all but debug builds.

  [`59f148b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/59f148bf6ea8c090d4d65ce5b135a71501e8ae22) Generate -fmacro-prefix-map to strip relative path from __FILE__.

  [`0eccbc8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/0eccbc8bf47073ab8ec1f13fed9865d0bd7c3635) Meson builds work on version 0.45.
- Allow any path to be passed to the `--test-path` option. (*Contributed by Andrey Sokolov. Reviewed by David Steele.*)
