  Pull request [#1764](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1764); Commit [`29b2a54`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/29b2a54fcc74457af5c63ffb505c5fbb24bb2ade)
- Fix compile error when `DEBUG_EXEC_TIME` is defined without `DEBUG`. (*Contributed by Andrey Sokolov. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Without `DEBUG` the `ASSERT()` that used the start time expanded to nothing, and the unused variable caused a compile error. The check now uses `CHECK()`.

  Pull request [#1775](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1775); Commit [`1a00ab1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1a00ab10033e9ac6e6f797627567e8b91e143432)

### v2.39 Release Notes

*Verify and File Bundling*

*Released May 16, 2022*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix error thrown from `FINALLY()` causing an infinite loop. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost.*)

  An error thrown in a `FINALLY()` block returned to the same block, where the error was likely thrown again and caused an infinite loop. `FINALLY()` now runs only once.

  [`9a271e9`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9a271e925c6239fbd3577f9827c34940e7e43ae4) Fix error thrown from FINALLY() causing an infinite loop.; Pull request [#1732](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1732)

  [`ef4c4ab`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ef4c4ab8525f81c9ed22c7e82ed12096e81703c5) Use variable instead of function to track FINALLY() state.; Pull request [#1744](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1744)
- Error on all lock failures except another process holding the lock. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson. Tested by Geir Råness. Reported by Geir Råness.*)

  `archive-get`/`archive-push` did not error when the lock could not be taken for another reason, e.g. permissions, and the async process was not launched. The user got a generic failure message and there was no async log. These failures are now errors.

  Issue [#1722](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1722); Pull request [#1730](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1730); Commit [`9629908`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/962990869454b15db058f4037dbc0b1b611fc95e)

**Features:**

- Backup file bundling for improved small file support. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson, Stefan Fercot, Chris Bandy.*)

  Bundling stores small files together in the repository to save time and space during backup, especially on object stores where creating each file is expensive. Enable it with `repo-bundle` and tune it with `repo-bundle-size` and `repo-bundle-limit`. Restore reads adjacent files in a bundle with a single read.

  [`4d2fef1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/4d2fef1c37d357965f0ffb9af0d7489f3be1371b) Remove redundant restoreFile() test and improve coverage.

  [`5141370`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/514137040e60e8fff0e7f42f43b493113abae5ff) Add limit parameter to ioCopyP().

  [`dca6da8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/dca6da86bfe84a0cda004df1140d125db1630c90) Optimize restore command for file bundling.; Issue [#1149](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1149); Pull request [#1683](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1683)

  [`0054677`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/0054677147121967889ead7feab0959fb19e39a6) Add bundle logging to backup command.

  [`3f66f42`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3f66f42ef9605a83c356e4c6eb3ef47bcb12c30d) Rename bundle-* options to repo-bundle-*.; Pull request [#1694](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1694)

  [`fe9fd2f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/fe9fd2ff2d778b86c0df159aabe7644f18f6479f) Disable repo-hardlink option when repo-bundle option is enabled.

  [`424008d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/424008d293661dfb1a167cfec2697def48057447) Allow files that become zero-length after the backup manifest is built.

  [`f405fc6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f405fc6ae281eebf4d61abb39963a9830fe2fa2f) Backup file bundling documentation.; Pull request [#1740](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1740)

  Development in [v2.38](#v238-release-notes):

  [`a79034a`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a79034ae2f6a8ec66b84c62ab6cd4042d2c1a249) Add read range to all storage drivers.; Pull request [#1619](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1619)

  [`b791f1c`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b791f1c82f53fa16866837d4544b58243c682f7f) Implement restore ownership without updating manifest internals.

  [`f84909f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f84909f85f7dfb202ac812c67665cc590a5acad4) Fix issue with Posix read offset handling after an error.

  [`4ca0590`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/4ca0590a51a665535fbba903b8f55e10557b606a) Refactor backupProcessQueue() to use BackupJobData struct.

  [`16559d9`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/16559d9e421c66596d21b047c8afe1b2c96ccd82) Use the PG_FILE_POSTMTRPID constant where appropriate.

  [`8c062e1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/8c062e1af824c3dc80ff94b3df8d433f95e88d0f) Remove primary flag from manifest.; Pull request [#1630](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1630)

  [`b0db4b8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b0db4b8ff00db913cb69308017a56155d6070830) Simplify base path mode in mock/all integration tests.

  [`4a73a02`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/4a73a0286387f3f2185f3f193f9c20ed0d7d926d) Simplify manifest defaults.; Pull request [#1631](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1631)

  [`575ae77`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/575ae77c0dee12b932469d479b2e45dbaf389737) Convert varNewUInt64() to VARUINT64() where possible in manifest.

  [`61ce586`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/61ce58692f3092b72833b6e9ee87037f45b3b702) Pack manifest file structs to save memory.; Pull request [#1626](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1626)

  [`ca13f11`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ca13f11b4a3858587c7055b0b2b4a8b396dd8451) Optimization for jsonFromStrInternal().

  [`e4df5b7`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e4df5b7d3846134832b9315e22786f679d63a46b) Simplify manifest file defaults.; Pull request [#1632](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1632)

  [`7dd657b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7dd657b7dd163ded9fbf7caef37eea7e08bc5a37) Simplify filename construction in command/verify module.

  [`34d6495`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/34d649579eb3bd1530aa99f0ed1879e7d3125424) Bundle files in the repository during backup.; Issue [#1149](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1149); Pull request [#1655](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1655)

  [`efc09db`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/efc09db7b9ece6e7b7f92538d56d6ab7b9798f8f) Limit files that can be bundled.; Issue [#1149](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1149); Pull request [#1662](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1662)

  [`e6e1122`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e6e1122dbcf5e667d683295f7e7e45de4bbf56bd) Pass file by reference in manifestFileAdd().
- Verify command to validate the contents of a repository. (*Contributed by Cynthia Shang, Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele, Stefan Fercot.*)

  The `verify` command checks that the backups and WAL in the repository are valid. Set `output=text` to print the results and `verbose` to include what was verified successfully along with errors.

  Pull request [#1607](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1607); Commit [`65d22e4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/65d22e43257d46ff6523ffbdb3af59e30deeaea5)
- PostgreSQL 15 support. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  PostgreSQL 15 removes exclusive backup, renames the backup start/stop functions, and drops `pg_database.datlastsysoid`, which pgBackRest no longer uses.

  [`692fe49`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/692fe496bdb5fa6dcffeb9f85b6188ceb1df707a) Remove dependency on pg_database.datlastsysoid.; Pull request [#1735](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1735)

  [`20782c8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/20782c88bc8d0842d547bd5f4fecdb292e69d1f7) PostgreSQL 15 support.; Pull request [#1736](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1736)
- Show backup percent complete in `info` output. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  `info` shows the percent complete of a running backup on the host where the backup is running. JSON output reports it as an integer, the percent times 100.

  [`d9088b2`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/d9088b2e2b16a89d29b967187a547976f96061fc) Show backup percent complete in info output.; Issue [#1490](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1490); Pull request [#1621](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1621)

  [`a6b1adb`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a6b1adb5fd533b4b9aca29646c8acf647d872959) Remove extraneous linefeed when writing a lock file.

  Development in [v2.38](#v238-release-notes):

  [`22734eb`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/22734eb376b3183924cb7794f9111c1eeb5d92d6) Add ioBufferReadNewOpen() and ioBufferWriteNewOpen().

  [`9b2f10d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9b2f10dbb49a8b869cef2c419d9957ed4c550016) Refactor lock code.; Pull request [#1643](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1643)
- Auto-select backup for `restore` command `--type=lsn`. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by Stefan Fercot, David Steele.*)

  With `type=lsn`, restore now selects the most recent backup that stopped at or before the `target` LSN.

  [`08d9e26`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/08d9e269c6f03e7797584e90c53eb0743b787f60) Refactor target type checking for clarity in restore module.

  [`d8d4132`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/d8d4132118edd7f93a5b221478c85c2d213c39dd) Auto-select backup for restore command --type=lsn.; Pull request [#1706](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1706)

  [`ea4d73f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ea4d73f375558c237fce696f20c19d1efef00bc1) Fix ordering of backup-lsn-stop field in command/restore unit test.
- Suppress existing WAL warning when `archive-mode-check` is disabled. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  With `archive-mode-check=n` and WAL pushed from multiple hosts, a WAL file already in the archive with the same checksum is expected and no longer logs a warning. A different checksum is still an error.

  Pull request [#1711](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1711); Commit [`aad7171`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/aad7171940afdb221a03825bee6eb2f1827289f1)
- Add AWS IMDSv2 support. (*Contributed by Nuno Pires. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  IMDSv2 protects the instance metadata that provides credentials for `repo-s3-key-type=auto`. pgBackRest falls back to IMDSv1 when the IMDSv2 token request fails.

  Issue [#1685](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1685); Pull request [#1691](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1691); Commit [`21cef09`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/21cef09dfd03c313531dc66ba9556164ae2835ab)

**Improvements:**

- Allow `repo-hardlink` option to be changed after full backup. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson.*)

  Changing `repo-hardlink` required a new full backup. It can now be changed at any time, e.g. to disable it for file bundling.

  Pull request [#1699](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1699); Commit [`7afaac0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7afaac0a3d0e43e5598d1abf548559dbe6329363)
- Increase precision of percent complete logging for `backup` and `restore`. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Percent complete is now logged with hundredths, which gives more frequent updates for large backups and restores.

  Pull request [#1679](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1679); Commit [`330e199`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/330e19900ee3415d920680cf6e6856bf5e8e9a69)
- Improve path validation for `repo-*` commands. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  A path may not contain `//`, a trailing `/` is removed, and an absolute path must be in the repository path.

  [`5ae84d5`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/5ae84d5e474e95dcc00c174aa5111815a5a70ba5) Improve path validation for repo-* commands.; Pull request [#1697](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1697)

  [`19dd015`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/19dd015d589ed3efd31a2a238249a150fb3748c1) Fix issues in improved path validation for repo-* commands.; Pull request [#1751](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1751)
- Improve `stop` command to honor `stanza` option. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele. Suggested by ragaoua.*)

  `stop` with `force` and `stanza` now terminates only the processes holding locks for that stanza. Before, it terminated processes for all stanzas.

  Issue [#1608](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1608); Pull request [#1676](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1676); Commit [`f7ab002`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f7ab002aa74dd2ca277123f7500018ae68a04c74)
- Improve error message for invalid `repo-azure-key`. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele. Suggested by Seth Daniel.*)

  With `repo-azure-key-type=shared`, `repo-azure-key` is now checked for valid base64.

  Issue [#1686](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1686); Pull request [#1688](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1688); Commit [`7c9208b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7c9208ba856a71833dc7d8f1cf8cb2b4bb4b7414)
- Add hint to check the log on `archive-get`/`archive-push` async error. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson.*)

  When the async process cannot write its status files, `archive-get`/`archive-push` times out with a generic error. The error now includes a hint with the path of the async log, which contains the actual error.

  Issue [#1722](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1722); Commit [`03c71aa`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/03c71aa606bc0dc89303b3907e7fcbd83ace79d6)
- Add `ClockError` for unexpected clock skew and timezone changes. (*Reviewed by Greg Sabino Mullane, Stefan Fercot. Suggested by Greg Sabino Mullane.*)

  Pull request [#1739](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1739); Commit [`5f8c9cd`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/5f8c9cd66a1b39a47acaddfbc3cf443ae8057519)
- Strip extensions from history manifest before showing in error message. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  When a new backup label was not later than the latest label from the backup history, the error showed the history manifest name, e.g. `20220504-222042F_20220504-222141I.manifest.gz`. It now shows only the label.

  Pull request [#1738](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1738); Commit [`b6bfd9f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b6bfd9f99d74b1f5e631cf6462b68342589d2b80)
- Add user:group to lock permission error. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson.*)

  Issue [#1722](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1722); Commit [`0055fa4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/0055fa40fee2636fed88699f52e9e67cd2803f71)
- Add hint when unable to find the primary cluster.

  The "unable to find primary cluster" error now includes a hint asking whether all available clusters are in recovery.

  Commit [`46b7b72`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/46b7b7287446d0bb5ed0963fd01c51fb2b08893f)

**Documentation Bug Fixes:**

- Fix incorrect reference to `stanza-update` in the user guide. (*Fixed by Abubakar Mohammed. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Commit [`3dd7960`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3dd796045160c4d4dbf70ec2800ad483867629f1)
- Fix example for `repo-gcs-key-type` option in configuration reference. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson.*)

  The example was `none`, which is not a valid value. It is now `auto`.

  Commit [`166039c`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/166039c0da1875581a08d41ab8a44adf95fa6db1)
- Fix `tls-server-auth` example and add clarifications. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson.*)

  The example was missing the client CN and is now `client-cn=stanza1`. The help also states that wildcards are not allowed for the client CN.

  Commit [`c222ce1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c222ce1a5f6f813188b7e87a3838d6dce656e0ff)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Simplify messaging around supported versions in the documentation. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot, Reid Thompson, Greg Sabino Mullane.*)

  The user guides listed PostgreSQL version ranges, which made it look like other versions were not supported, e.g. PostgreSQL 12 on RHEL. The ranges were removed and the introduction now explains which versions the user guides cover.

  Pull request [#1742](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1742); Commit [`eefa0b1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/eefa0b161a6a02b7843e18455443a4bce41cd4b9)
- Add option type descriptions. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The configuration reference now describes each option type, e.g. the units for size options, rather than repeating the description for each option.

  Pull request [#1747](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1747); Commit [`a913113`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a913113fda5886ffd2ff136cdd52a3f469eca026)
- Add FAQ about backup types and restore speed. (*Contributed by David Christensen. Reviewed by Reid Thompson.*)

  A new FAQ explains that the backup type affects only the backup and not how fast a restore runs.

  Pull request [#1731](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1731); Commit [`50d409a`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/50d409a812be577f2c296f3d6096cf97fab049ff)
- Document required base branch for pull requests. (*Contributed by David Christensen. Reviewed by Reid Thompson.*)

  The contributing guide now states that pull requests must be based on the `integration` branch.

  Pull request [#1733](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1733); Commit [`cc5b061`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cc5b0614894e1b5ef78f1ceba3bc8e4760a40a24)

### v2.38 Release Notes

*Minor Bug Fixes and Improvements*

*Released March 6, 2022*

**IMPORTANT NOTE**: Repository size reported by the `info` command is now entirely based on what pgBackRest has written to storage. Previously, in certain cases, pgBackRest could detect if additional compression was being applied by the storage but this is no longer supported.

**Bug Fixes:**

- Retry errors in S3 batch file delete. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson. Reported by Alex Richman.*)

  A failed batch delete was retried, but errors for individual files in the batch were not, and pgBackRest could terminate during expire or when removing a backup that could not be resumed. Files that fail in a batch are now deleted individually with the usual HTTP retries.

  Issue [#1650](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1650); Pull request [#1653](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1653); Commit [`551e5bc`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/551e5bc6f67c91cf1e11da0cefe24f9650353adc)
- Allow case-insensitive matching of HTTP `connection` header values. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson. Reported by Rémi Vidier.*)

  The HTTP specification allows `connection` header values in any case, e.g. `Close`.

  Issue [#1638](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1638); Pull request [#1675](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1675); Commit [`b33cabe`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b33cabe08c33e29eb8aef65dd46b2c1f3cf2d186)

**Features:**

- Add support for AWS S3 server-side encryption using KMS. (*Contributed by Christoph Berg. Reviewed by David Steele. Tested by Tharindu Amila.*)

  Set `repo-s3-kms-key-id` to encrypt objects with a key managed by AWS KMS.

  Issue [#1430](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1430); Pull request [#1567](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1567); Commit [`3097acd`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3097acd73a21b630aa5352be57a82b031930f96b)
- Add `archive-missing-retry` option. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  By default `archive-get` retries a WAL segment it already reported as missing, which ignores missing notifications left in the spool path by a prior restore. With `archive-missing-retry=n`, PostgreSQL can detect the end of the archive and switch to streaming even while WAL is being archived. The spool path must then be empty when recovery starts.

  Pull request [#1642](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1642); Commit [`10038db`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/10038db9c9f2b4753c3821e0b246fa8626527184)
- Add backup type filter to `info` command. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Set `type` to show only backups of that type, e.g. `type=full`.

  Issue [#600](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/600); Pull request [#1651](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1651); Commit [`b26097f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b26097f8d8a7e2af6e9a21576994cbb47110c6f4)

**Improvements:**

- Retry on page validation failure during `backup`. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost, David Christensen.*)

  A page that fails checksum validation is now read again. It is reported as an error only when it has not changed. A changed page is being written by PostgreSQL, and the WAL contains a full page image. Zero pages are now checked to be all zeroes rather than only by the `pd_upper` field.

  [`b489707`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b4897077937ee4571ba719276a44d5db0a75510e) Move command/backup-common tests in the command/backup module.

  [`9eec98c`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9eec98c61302121134d2067326dbd2cd0f2f0b9c) Retry on page checksum validation failure during backup.; Issue [#952](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/952); Pull request [#1667](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1667)

  [`53f1b25`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/53f1b2520434221c01caf92e052403a28c455c1b) Improve validation of zero pages.; Issue [#952](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/952); Pull request [#1669](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1669)
- Handle TLS servers that do not close connections gracefully. (*Reviewed by David Christensen, Stephen Frost. Tested by Rémi Vidier.*)

  Some servers close the socket rather than the TLS connection. When a response has no content length or chunked encoding and its content can be parsed, the closed socket is now treated as EOF. These servers stopped working in v2.35.

  Issue [#1638](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1638); Pull request [#1677](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1677); Commit [`59a5373`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/59a5373cf85e4ad0057b592bfe855495384dfcbd)
- Add backup LSNs to `info` command output. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The start and stop LSNs of a backup are shown with `set` and in JSON output, for use with LSN-based Point-in-Time Recovery.

  Pull request [#1610](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1610); Commit [`d866dd5`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/d866dd5c297dc046581134a108032cf9694cae0e)
- Automatically strip trailing slashes for `repo-ls` paths. (*Contributed by David Christensen. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  A trailing slash on the path made `repo-ls` show no content on some repository storage types.

  Pull request [#1673](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1673); Commit [`6320712`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/632071232344a1160ef932d6e0d65eb3149ed226)
- Do not retry fatal errors. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson.*)

  Retrying fatal errors, especially out of memory errors, may cause lockups. Assertion and memory errors are now reported without retrying.

  [`8573a2d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/8573a2df14d0bfd5578333b68147cc12c93c6f96) Improve protocol module error test for protocolClientFree().

  [`8d0cce6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/8d0cce66f83abc1ff02a985ec3f0d7e285c09d72) Use normal error for protocol module error retry test.

  [`8046f06`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/8046f0630715015d5adcced570c54e9a183c0e73) Do not retry fatal errors.; Pull request [#1658](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1658)
- Remove support for PostgreSQL `8.3`/`8.4`. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson, Stefan Fercot.*)

  There is no evidence that these versions are still in use and supporting them costs development and test time. Use an older version of pgBackRest to back up or migrate from these versions.

  Pull request [#1611](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1611); Commit [`bb4b30d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/bb4b30ddd3f80c246c565eb4769efc82b9509648)
- Remove logic that tried to determine additional file system compression. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson, Stefan Fercot.*)

  After a file was copied, pgBackRest checked its size on disk to detect compression by the file system, e.g. ZFS. This was untested, slow with many small files, and blocked file bundling. The repository size in `info` is now what pgBackRest wrote.

  Pull request [#1652](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1652); Commit [`cb630ff`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cb630ffe3b5c819b5e97ac11d5974000bd44d632)

**Documentation Bug Fixes:**

- Move `repo` options in TLS documentation to the `global` section. (*Reported by Anton Kurochkin.*)

  The user guide set these options in the stanza section, which works in some cases but is not best practice.

  Commit [`53de3e3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/53de3e3aeb71f8209f56167941df35bbfcb7cdc9)
- Remove unused `backup-standby` option from stanza commands. (*Reported by Stefan Fercot.*)

  `stanza-create` and `stanza-upgrade` listed `backup-standby` in the command reference and help, but did not use it.

  Commit [`5cbb4a3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/5cbb4a3e6b13598b90cae46fad28c31604f9f094)
- Fix typos in help and release notes. (*Fixed by Daniel Gustafsson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  [`cacfdd9`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cacfdd94d74307285b1b590ec0977d7db3142d0d) Fix typos in help and release notes.; Pull request [#1671](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1671)

  [`5c332a0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/5c332a0b100d5a5180b3d95bd90d4d7b521b42db) Fix release note attribution in cacfdd94 and f798458e.

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Add aliveness check to systemd service configuration. (*Suggested by Yogesh Sharma.*)

  The `pgbackrest.service` example now checks that the server is still running shortly after it starts. Before, a server that failed to start reported no error while systemd retried in the background.

  Commit [`f1bdf3e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f1bdf3e04b07dc9a2eaa1fdbe5278cc18371e868)
- Add FAQ explaining WAL archive suffix. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The suffix on a WAL file in the repository is its SHA1 checksum, which is used to verify the file. It cannot be omitted.

  Issue [#1485](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1485); Issue [#1663](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1663); Pull request [#1664](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1664); Commit [`98d525d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/98d525dba43ba259ff7989746b8e5be3382c189d)
- Note that replications slots are not restored. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele, Stefan Fercot. Suggested by Christophe Courtois.*)

  Issue [#1585](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1585); Pull request [#1601](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1601); Commit [`a82f017`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a82f0179cdd5742e5c5b1e0bdfd23166753fdb38)

### v2.37 Release Notes

*TLS Server*

*Released January 3, 2022*

**IMPORTANT NOTE**: If the `restore` command is unable to find a backup that matches a specified time target then an error will be thrown, whereas before a warning was logged.

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix `restore` delta link mapping when path/file already exists. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson. Reported by Younes Alhroub.*)

  When `link-map` or `link-all` mapped a path or file that already existed to a link, a delta restore left the existing path or file in place. It is now replaced by the link.

  Issue [#1588](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1588); Pull request [#1589](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1589); Commit [`a73fe4e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a73fe4eb966f9685f6e4179c397a10c1e7f15f19)
- Fix socket leak on connection retries. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson. Reported by James Coleman.*)

  In optimized builds the socket of a failed connection attempt was not closed, and each retry leaked a socket.

  Issue [#1593](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1593); Pull request [#1594](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1594); Commit [`615bdff`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/615bdff4030a31bfedfe7df04676e3948ec9c2c0)

**Features:**

- Add TLS server. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost, Reid Thompson. Tested by Andrew L'Ecuyer.*)

  The `server` command starts a TLS server that can be used instead of SSH to reach repository and PostgreSQL hosts. Set `repo-host-type=tls` or `pg-host-type=tls` on the client, and configure certificates and client authorization with the `tls-server-*` options. `SIGHUP` reloads the configuration and `SIGTERM` stops the server.

  [`2d963ce`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/2d963ce9471808172f879916c3f3accc35f14d56) Rename server-start command to server.

  [`7b3ea88`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7b3ea883c7c010aafbeb14d150d073a113b703e4) Add SIGTERM and SIGHUP handling to TLS server.; Pull request [#1572](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1572)

  [`f06101d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f06101de77a980c7e4115762f2fc301280aa4127) Add TLS server documentation.; Pull request [#1597](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1597)

  Development in [v2.36](#v236-release-notes):

  [`ccc255d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ccc255d3e05d8ce2b6ac251d1498f71b04098a86) Add TLS Server.; Pull request [#1504](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1504)

  [`13d4559`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/13d4559708819787ad05be6f37ec0badb0eccae5) Check return value of getsockopt().

  Development in [v2.35](#v235-release-notes):

  [`947c7a8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/947c7a84cb37d09ab925785ca7670c970e32b8a3) Refactor cmdLocal()/cmdRemote() to accept a ProtocolServer object.

  [`0999de0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/0999de0279fabf35af80396a9a8d35546ec02d53) Move noop from protocolClientNew() to local/remote initialization.
- Add `--cmd` option. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by Stefan Fercot, David Steele. Suggested by Virgile CREVON.*)

  Set `cmd` to the pgBackRest executable used in commands that pgBackRest writes, e.g. `restore_command` in `recovery.conf`.

  Issue [#1537](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1537); Pull request [#1540](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1540); Commit [`2a57647`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/2a576477b316238473525e56bc8fc8ea5790455f)

**Improvements:**

- Check archive immediately after backup start. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson, David Christensen.*)

  A backup failed because WAL archiving was not working only after all files were copied. The archive is now checked right after the backup starts. On an idle system a restore point is created to switch the WAL segment.

  Issue [#152](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/152); Pull request [#1570](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1570); Commit [`1f14f45`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1f14f45dfb0d1677a695719381cbd5a8a3c6c986)
- Add timeline and checkpoint checks to backup. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot, Reid Thompson.*)

  The backup now checks that `pg_control` has the checkpoint and timeline of the backup start, and that a standby is following the primary, i.e. it is on the same timeline and its checkpoint is not later than the backup checkpoint.

  Pull request [#1581](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1581); Commit [`49145d7`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/49145d72bac16498cdbf5eeb3cd6128ea0be0667)
- Check that clusters are alive and correctly configured during a backup. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  A backup now fails as soon as a cluster stops or the standby is promoted. Before, a stopped primary was detected only at the end of the backup and a promoted standby was not detected.

  Pull request [#1584](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1584); Commit [`bd2ba80`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/bd2ba802db11c505ec69943fa81b2b379073fbf4)
- Error when `restore` is unable to find a backup to match the time target. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson, Douglas J Hunley. Suggested by Douglas J Hunley.*)

  This was a warning that was easy to miss, and a lot of time could be lost restoring a backup that cannot reach the target.

  Issue [#1582](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1582); Pull request [#1586](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1586); Commit [`be4ac39`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/be4ac3923cb77873da298a30aca5d847b3c635af)
- Parse protocol/port in S3/Azure endpoints. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  `repo-s3-endpoint`, `repo-azure-endpoint`, and `repo-storage-host` may now include a protocol and port, e.g. `https://host:9000`.

  Issue [#1560](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1560); Pull request [#1591](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1591); Commit [`6a12458`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6a12458440168f13cb05d70f36ea54b1860e390c)
- Add warning when `checkpoint_timeout` exceeds `db-timeout`. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  With `start-fast=n`, a backup can time out waiting for the checkpoint when `checkpoint_timeout` is greater than `db-timeout`. The backup now warns in this case.

  Pull request [#1564](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1564); Commit [`6723305`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/672330593789f07aaad90bbafcd2597cbc602686)
- Add verb to HTTP error output. (*Contributed by Christoph Berg. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Pull request [#1566](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1566); Commit [`c38e2d3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c38e2d31709804eb4b9125a15ad84c8fc813f366)
- Allow y/n arguments for boolean command-line options. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Pull request [#1571](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1571); Commit [`1a0560d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1a0560d363d28737befb8c222647783d4fc2ca29)
- Make backup size logging exactly match `info` command output. (*Contributed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele. Suggested by Mahomed Hussein.*)

  The logged backup size now includes only the files copied, plus `backup_label` and `tablespace_map`. Before, e.g. an incremental backup after a switchover logged the size of every file checked.

  [`6e63576`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6e635764a66278d5a8c2b4d30b23063bc3923067) Match backup log size with size reported by info command.; Issue [#766](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/766); Pull request [#1562](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1562)

  [`afe77e7`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/afe77e76e0adf948138d797e227a6f4c7d47c2eb) Update contributor for 6e635764.

  [`1e77fc3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1e77fc3d75490b7a1b6a0b31be9298c995ec672f) Include backup_label and tablespace_map file sizes in log output.; Pull request [#1568](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1568)

  [`a3d7a23`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a3d7a23a9d90611a3d31947598fbea240b250710) Use infoBackupDataByLabel() to log backup size.; Pull request [#1573](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1573)

  [`dcb4f09`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/dcb4f09d8315e92c0877b589f3fa9b7f0fa65f93) Revert changes to backupFilePut() made in 1e77fc3d.

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Display size option default and allowed values with appropriate units. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson.*)

  [`cff961e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cff961ede7e41fa8035ffe7451a22eb5ea0e46c1) Centralize logic to build value lists during config rendering.

  [`038abaa`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/038abaa71d816cc87b382bd81d3df62ddec9455a) Display size option default and allowed values with appropriate units.; Pull request [#1557](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1557)
- Fix typos and improve documentation for the `tablespace-map-all` option. (*Reviewed by Reid Thompson. Suggested by Reid Thompson.*)

  Commit [`df89eff`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/df89eff429e9b8fbc68d9e9895badf9719fd31d2)
- Remove obsolete statement about future multi-repository support. (*Suggested by David Christensen.*)

  The documentation stated that only one repository was allowed, but multiple repositories have been supported since v2.33.

  Commit [`dea7524`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/dea752477ab8e812cdbd717eb2091baf3f5d0906)

### v2.36 Release Notes

*Minor Bug Fixes and Improvements*

*Released November 1, 2021*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Allow "global" as a stanza prefix. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Reported by Younes Alhroub.*)

  A stanza name that begins with `global`, e.g. `global_stanza`, was rejected because the config parser treated it as a global section.

  Issue [#1524](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1524); Pull request [#1525](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1525); Commit [`498902e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/498902e885c9d7a44648ccc01b20454094d5b742)
- Fix segfault on invalid GCS key file. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost. Reported by Henrik Feldt.*)

  A GCS key file without the client email, private key, or token URI caused a segfault. It now causes an error.

  Issue [#1536](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1536); Commit [`2cea005`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/2cea005f740d640290a9948595f5933833e30e7d)

**Improvements:**

- Allow `link-map` option to create new links. (*Reviewed by Don Seiler, Stefan Fercot, Chris Bandy. Suggested by Don Seiler.*)

  `link-map` could only remap links that were in the backup. It can now create a new link for any path or file in the backup.

  [`d89a677`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/d89a67776cfbb7b3047dbe297cc0c768e5c670e8) Refactor restoreManifestMap() tests in the command/restore unit.

  [`cf1a575`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cf1a57518fe3230886509f59fcec9c9a81e6513c) Refactor restoreManifestMap() to be driven by link-map.

  [`68c5f3e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/68c5f3eaf18fc9bd10fde15a323890bcdcbf4534) Allow link-map option to create new links.; Issue [#1409](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1409); Pull request [#1520](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1520)
- Increase max index allowed for `pg`/`repo` options to 256. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)

  The maximum index was 8 for `pg` options and 4 for `repo` options. Both are now 256, e.g. `pg256-path`.

  [`02b06aa`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/02b06aa4957cf3fe64ceb7a44cc1893bff12fe99) Increase max index allowed for pg/repo options to 256.; Pull request [#1481](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1481)

  [`71047a9`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/71047a9d6d1eea71b3fbd430983541a54049cc69) Use strncpy() to limit characters copied to optionName.
- Add `WebIdentity` authentication for AWS S3. (*Reviewed by James Callahan, Reid Thompson, Andrew L'Ecuyer. Tested by Benjamin Blattberg.*)

  Set `repo-s3-key-type=web-id` to get credentials automatically in an EKS environment.

  [`cfd8233`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/cfd823355af2ac99f30c5e1393a121c6dbf622b7) Refactor S3 storage driver for additional auth methods.

  [`3879bc6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3879bc69b888daa04d2ca98a2d1219cf22519ddc) Add WebIdentity authentication for AWS S3.; Issue [#1203](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1203); Pull request [#1527](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1527)

  [`1152f7a`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1152f7a7d64e69eed1d9e74b48a308f6c742c28a) Fix mismatched parameters in tlsClientNew() call.
- Add `repo-azure-uri-style` option.

  Set `repo-azure-uri-style` to `host` (default) or `path` to select the URI style for Azure. Azurite uses host-style URIs when the endpoint looks like a multipart host name.

  Commit [`096829b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/096829b3b257444162417662612e80d8cb2ac6ec)
- Report backup file validation errors in `backup.info`. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Errors found during a backup, e.g. invalid page checksums, were shown only by `info` with `set`. A flag in `backup.info` now shows in text and JSON output that a backup has errors. The details still require `set`.

  Issue [#1484](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1484); Pull request [#1508](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1508); Commit [`34f7873`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/34f78734325743b4e34cb39224852e6debf49750)
- Add recovery start time to online backup restore log. (*Reviewed by Tom Swartz, Stefan Fercot. Suggested by Tom Swartz.*)

  Restore now logs the time that recovery will start from, which shows how much WAL must be replayed.

  Issue [#1507](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1507); Pull request [#1518](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1518); Commit [`9e79f0e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9e79f0e64b661e944ce2b3897c366feea1544ac2)
- Report original error and retries on local job failure. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  When a local job fails after its retries, the error now reports the first error with its stack trace, followed by the retry errors. Before, only the last error was reported, which was often a cascade failure such as the protocol being out of sync.

  [`136d309`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/136d309dd4bc1ada9f3d775f036b62292fda390b) Allow stack trace to be specified for errorInternalThrow().

  [`ae40ed6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ae40ed6ec9cf77e577518b128518a5763767f589) Add jobRetry parameter to HRN_CFG_LOAD().

  [`6af827c`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6af827cbb1e78cd4c5d649ed4cb24c49a7204b8f) Report original error and retries on local job failure.; Pull request [#1521](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1521)
- Rename page checksum error to error list in info text output. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot.*)

  The label in `info` text output is now "error list" rather than "page checksum error". A file may have an alignment error rather than a page checksum error.

  Pull request [#1519](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1519); Commit [`9346895`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9346895f5b61627b50d431f09447e08d8a50caa8)
- Add hints to standby replay timeout message. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang, Stefan Fercot. Suggested by Leigh Downs.*)

  The timeout waiting for the standby to replay now has hints to check that replication is running and to disable `backup-standby` to back up from the primary.

  Issue [#1434](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1434); Commit [`b7ef12a`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b7ef12a76f219881d0b24c715592af96fe5c9b8f)

**Documentation Bug Fixes:**

- Fix incorrect host name in user guide. (*Fixed by Reid Thompson. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The replication setup used the IP address of pg-primary in `pg_hba.conf` rather than the IP address of pg-standby.

  Pull request [#1544](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1544); Commit [`1cb8ae1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1cb8ae15de5b4276682bdd9825ca97012cd43855)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Improve option documentation.

  The help for `buffer-size` now describes all the operations that use it, the descriptions of `cmd-ssh`, `repo-host-cmd`, and `pg-host-cmd` are consistent, and `archive-header-check` moved to the archive section of the configuration reference.

  [`3763624`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/376362475e3b3b13b70313d9002d4ff8b25b4b40) Move archive-header-check option to the archive reference section.

  [`e1f6c06`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e1f6c066b3da11fd21b1155c90370c3fa2da06b7) Improve documentation for buffer-size option.

  [`fa564ee`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/fa564ee1969229b5cf60d2479d8ace85325f4db3) Improve documentation for cmd-ssh, repo-host-cmd, pg-host-cmd options.

### v2.35 Release Notes

*Binary Protocol*

*Released August 23, 2021*

**IMPORTANT NOTE**: The log level for copied files in the `backup`/`restore` commands has been changed to `detail`. This makes the `info` log level less noisy but if these messages are required then set the log level for the `backup`/`restore` commands to `detail`

**Bug Fixes:**

- Detect errors in S3 multi-part upload finalize. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Tested by Marco Montagna. Reported by Marco Montagna, Lev Kokotov, Anderson A. Mallmann.*)

  Completing a multi-part upload can fail even when S3 returns HTTP success. A response without an `ETag` is now treated as a failure, and the job is retried.

  [`8e1807c`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/8e1807cdbe08a6e059ff79220247cf4b3c058979) Detect errors in S3 multi-part upload finalize.; Issue [#1116](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1116); Issue [#1290](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1290); Issue [#1433](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1433); Pull request [#1438](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1438)

  [`25508a4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/25508a48e0d3b0a3eabc6d1fe858b9c3dc12dc86) Add contributors missed in 8e187cdb.
- Fix detection of circular symlinks. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Reported by Rohit Raveendran.*)

  Links were followed before they were checked, and a circular link sent the manifest build into endless recursion and a crash.

  Issue [#1447](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1447); Pull request [#1452](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1452); Commit [`43b8746`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/43b874628c86ec4fe297dbd5cab5bd3f62b8639b)
- Only pass selected `repo` options to the remote. (*Reviewed by David Christensen, Cynthia Shang. Reported by Greg Sabino Mullane, David Christensen.*)

  Options for other repositories could cause conflicts on the remote, which uses exactly one repository.

  Pull request [#1480](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1480); Commit [`935de1e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/935de1e7a06d60415c16cad137875b49346337c6)

**Improvements:**

- Binary protocol. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)

  Local and remote processes now communicate with the binary pack format rather than JSON. Packs are more compact, faster to render and parse, and strictly typed. Each message is written in one place, which makes an error less likely to leave the protocol in a bad state, and messages have no size limit.

  [`6a1c033`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6a1c0337ddee9900dda58b7d2fa74d82c0586ed2) Binary protocol.; Pull request [#1424](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1424)

  [`2d5cbfc`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/2d5cbfc73142fa99df609aa23b64db5342e73c88) Fix memory leaks introduced by the binary protocol in 6a1c0337.

  [`f716cb6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f716cb6f4f9765baa03f71b5563786d79d9f04a2) Fix use after free introduced by the binary protocol in 6a1c0337.

  [`4fb6384`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/4fb6384f10f6cd26c422ab9b24feb5ae0ebeeee9) Fix more memory leaks introduced by the binary protocol in 6a1c0337.
- Automatically create data directory on `restore`. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele. Suggested by Chris Bandy.*)

  A check migrated from Perl prevented restore from creating the data directory.

  Issue [#1445](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1445); Pull request [#1454](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1454); Commit [`056a307`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/056a3070e0dba299389c7b5049878d40dd91ec9a)
- Allow `restore` `--type=lsn`. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Suggested by James Coleman.*)

  Set `type=lsn` and an LSN in `target` to recover to an LSN on PostgreSQL >= 10.

  Issue [#1339](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1339); Pull request [#1462](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1462); Commit [`563d117`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/563d117967ab9770f9f646c6186d0bc37121611f)
- Change level of `backup`/`restore` copied file logging to detail. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Suggested by Jens Wilke.*)

  This makes the `info` log level less noisy. Set the log level to `detail` to see each copied file.

  Issue [#1277](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1277); Pull request [#1455](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1455); Commit [`849ab34`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/849ab343aa10d66fbbd27cad407eff981c186d06)
- Loop while waiting for checkpoint LSN to reach replay LSN. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele. Suggested by Fatih Mencutekin.*)

  On a standby the checkpoint LSN can lag slightly behind the replay LSN until `pg_control` is updated. The backup now keeps checking rather than failing.

  Issue [#1450](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1450); Pull request [#1460](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1460); Commit [`8fac1ac`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/8fac1ac50d28557d162fbb251fbbdcc3c9fb950c)
- Log `backup` file total and `restore` size/file total. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)

  The backup size is now logged at the end of the backup and includes files added to the manifest after the main copy, e.g. `backup_label` and WAL. Restore now logs its size and file total.

  Pull request [#1489](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1489); Commit [`a0bdfa4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a0bdfa436c9e1c2723be905ebe5cef7809a26244)

**Documentation Bug Fixes:**

- Fix incorrect host names in user guide. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Reported by Greg Sabino Mullane.*)

  Some examples showed `pg1` rather than the host name, and the install block installed packages on the build host no matter which host was specified.

  Pull request [#1479](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1479); Commit [`155ef85`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/155ef85013088a1150a4c15b04af36420d06e9c3)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Update contributing documentation and add pull request template. (*Contributed by Cynthia Shang. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The contributing guide now has a table of contents and sections on understanding test output and submitting a pull request. GitHub now shows a template for new pull requests.

  [`e3d05e2`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e3d05e2b4aa80149219b6d49a3136b34fa268127) Update contributing documentation and add pull request template.; Pull request [#1469](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1469)

  [`71b654f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/71b654fc29497319862fe01c3917bbc353cc6dd9) Fix links and update child process example.; Pull request [#1487](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1487)
- Rearrange backup documentation in user guide. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)

  The "Fast Start Option" and "Archive Timeout" sections moved to "Quick Start - Perform Backup". The "Automatic Stop Option" section, which applies only to PostgreSQL <= 9.6, was removed.

  Pull request [#1488](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1488); Commit [`6ab18dc`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6ab18dc0fa42885aea8eb9405ab0a943c2d49a71)
- Clarify `restore` `--type` behavior in command reference. (*Contributed by Cynthia Shang. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The reference now states that `set` must be specified when the target is before the latest backup and `type` is not `time`.

  Commit [`a6691c6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a6691c6f615f90195aeb6c1c6657a6afd79a10d3)
- Fix documentation and comment typos. (*Contributed by Eric Radman. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Pull request [#1446](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1446); Commit [`23bdc3d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/23bdc3deb62a622713064df3b0777110ebba8723)

**Test Suite Improvements:**

- Add check for test path inside repo path. (*Reviewed by Greg Sabino Mullane. Suggested by Greg Sabino Mullane.*)

  A test path inside the repository path caused strange failures because the repository is copied into the test path. `test.pl` now errors in this case.

  Issue [#1472](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1472); Pull request [#1474](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1474); Commit [`e32f9e1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e32f9e146b11389e3d16feba9665360b73737d66)
- Add CodeQL static code analysis. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)

  CodeQL now runs in CI. Issues it found were fixed, e.g. `gmtime()`/`localtime()` were replaced with `gmtime_r()`/`localtime_r()`.

  Pull request [#1457](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1457); Commit [`3c8819e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3c8819e10f9eff89195566e7e02f349373f5c4c7)
- Update tests to use standard patterns. (*Contributed by Cynthia Shang. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  [`3f9fbc3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3f9fbc3c24eab48e7ae2871484ea01273049e601) Update command/expire test to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1420](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1420)

  [`03021c6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/03021c6a17f1374e84ef42614fa1dd2a6be4b64d) Update command/stanza test to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1426](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1426)

  [`8dd882b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/8dd882badee76b038dc8b494a1112eb3e18ef678) Update info/infoArchive test to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1436](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1436)

  [`ff95edd`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ff95eddc369597c76a9ea70ffbada493ed4d1951) Update command/verify test to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1435](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1435)

  [`17c9ed0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/17c9ed0ef0380fb14fba9a5299b6618ca8c92c2e) Update command/info test to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1432](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1432)

  [`bffb43e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/bffb43ea3fe00f445a2fc3c44a07f105cee65b4b) Update command/check test to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1431](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1431)

  [`fb2d111`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/fb2d111dcd75021313a64a348959482339bf7f2a) Update info/infoBackup test to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1437](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1437)

  [`065a8c8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/065a8c8454d2acdd72c0c71a24d6b8bc09669e18) Replace defines with string constants in unit tests.; Pull request [#1440](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1440)

  [`c620811`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c6208113bfce9d637ca3fd0b35ab28a819c1a83e) Add timeModified parameter to HRN_STORAGE_PUT() macro.

  [`6a90d46`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6a90d469094ee2697ae4c932349756a54136799d) Update info/manifest test to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1444](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1444)

  [`62e8d97`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/62e8d97af03e7628d5710b17aad1cc31f83d7e69) Update command/archive tests to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1449](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1449)

  [`8bb0b28`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/8bb0b28455d59ef884eab7ab25a91f988adbab81) Update command/control and command/command tests to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1456](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1456)

  [`f2ec4e0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f2ec4e00a4c4c388a8f1b094381fd566a3dc9aa7) Add cipher parameters to TEST_STORAGE_GET() macro.

  [`4ad0bbd`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/4ad0bbda53b34688146f14d130cf0e53c8dd650f) Update command/backup tests to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1463](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1463)

  [`117ffe8`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/117ffe88965fd60ce3dcb328e9d28503870af05a) Update command/help tests to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1464](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1464)

  [`ebe5848`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ebe5848494d4e37a9c86f1fafb5b3453c1f425a6) Update command/local and command/remote tests to use standard patterns.; Pull request [#1466](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1466)

### v2.34 Release Notes

*PostgreSQL 14 Support*

*Released June 7, 2021*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix issues with leftover spool files from a prior `restore`. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang, Stefan Fercot. Tested by Floris van Nee. Reported by Floris van Nee.*)

  A notification left in the spool path by a prior restore could make `archive-get` report a WAL segment as missing. When this notification is found on the first check, it is now removed and the async process is run again. An error is reported when the async process does not respond, rather than reporting the WAL segment as missing.

  Issue [#1357](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1357); Pull request [#1371](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1371); Commit [`f45e76f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f45e76fa2e8ce0f62317a74fdd51083d52659cd5)
- Fix issue when checking links for large numbers of tablespaces. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Tested by Avinash Vallarapu. Reported by Avinash Vallarapu.*)

  The link check used a lot of memory with many tablespaces and could crash. The new check is much faster, uses far less memory, and also detects duplicate file links.

  Issue [#1354](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1354); Pull request [#1356](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1356); Commit [`eba013b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/eba013b49b850e53d59795ac6358fcb3381048a3)
- Free no longer needed remotes so they do not timeout during `restore`. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Reported by Francisco Miguel Biete Banon.*)

  The connections to remotes were held for the whole restore and could time out, which logged a harmless but distracting warning at the end of a long restore. They are now closed after the manifest is loaded.

  Issue [#1368](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1368); Pull request [#1374](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1374); Commit [`06fa185`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/06fa18582a405417c8ab4ba667cb01a13a5c3103)
- Fix `help` when a valid option is invalid for the specified command. (*Reviewed by Stefan Fercot. Reported by Cynthia Shang.*)

  `help` segfaulted when asked about an option that is not valid for the command.

  Pull request [#1375](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1375); Commit [`45f8355`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/45f83558ea862c6f496a27b886e1160e6303bbe8)

**Features:**

- Add PostgreSQL 14 support. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)

  PostgreSQL 14 needs no code changes from PostgreSQL 13. The catalog version may now change during the PostgreSQL beta/rc period without requiring a new pgBackRest release.

  Pull request [#1413](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1413); Commit [`2452c4d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/2452c4d5a4c230b8e3c33d3bddf69e56dfbec284)
- Add automatic GCS authentication for GCE instances. (*Reviewed by Jan Wieck. Tested by Daniel Farina.*)

  Set `repo-gcs-key-type=auto` to get the authentication token from the instance metadata on a GCE instance.

  [`7b71604`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7b71604defa02d5ed9b7aa3407961cb59a52cc45) Use existing variable for GCS test server port.

  [`26fefa6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/26fefa6aeeae90bbb572ea57b605fb8fe526f1a0) Refactor GCS token response parsing into a separate function.

  [`9af0331`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9af033194abc4b1cd3f06c3a97fff71c966c3827) Add automatic GCS authentication for GCE instances.; Pull request [#1395](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1395)
- Add `repo-retention-history` option to expire backup history. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by Cynthia Shang, David Steele.*)

  Set the number of days of backup history to keep. By default all history is kept, and history for backups still in the repository is never expired. When a full backup manifest expires, the manifests of its differential and incremental backups expire as well.

  Pull request [#1389](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1389); Commit [`5b332b2`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/5b332b22b508d896abb64fa6abe26c78c83a29bc)
- Add `db-exclude` option. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)

  Restore excludes the specified databases by writing them as sparse, zeroed files. PostgreSQL can still perform recovery, and the databases can be dropped after recovery. With `db-include`, `db-exclude` applies only to the system databases `template0`, `template1`, and `postgres`.

  Issue [#986](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/986); Pull request [#1337](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1337); Commit [`292f836`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/292f836f1243e51d102bb4be202e7b21a7a386b1)

**Improvements:**

- Change archive expiration logging from detail to info level. (*Contributed by Cynthia Shang. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Pull request [#1416](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1416); Commit [`aceb956`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/aceb956815a87f0c19ea171de25d2c3842a6d806)
- Remove stanza archive spool path on `restore`. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang, Stefan Fercot.*)

  Files in the spool path could interfere with the new cluster, e.g. old `archive-push` acknowledgements could make it skip archiving WAL.

  Issue [#1357](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1357); Pull request [#1386](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1386); Commit [`320c6e1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/320c6e1aad2cc9a8dabecb2bc91a9b34464de6ec)
- Do not write files atomically or sync paths during `backup` copy. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost, Stefan Fercot, Cynthia Shang.*)

  Atomic writes are not needed since checksums are validated when a failed backup is resumed, and all paths are synced at the end of the backup.

  Pull request [#1373](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1373); Commit [`aa72c19`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/aa72c19a831fb0dd15a50a003fbaad427f335353)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Update contributing documentation. (*Contributed by Cynthia Shang. Reviewed by David Steele, Stefan Fercot.*)

  The contributing guide has more examples of how to write code, add configuration options, and test.

  Pull request [#1400](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1400); Commit [`68faf14`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/68faf1482a42ea14f5b00a91e19bd5aedec275b8)
- Consolidate RHEL/CentOS user guide into a single document. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)

  The second guide existed mainly to document `stop-auto` for PostgreSQL <= 9.5, which is now EOL. The option is still documented in the reference.

  Pull request [#1405](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1405); Commit [`ccac75e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/ccac75e7de8754b39bb691ff8830a2839ea7a285)
- Clarify that `repo-s3-role` is not an `ARN`. (*Contributed by Isaac Yuen. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Pull request [#1365](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1365); Commit [`5bf1606`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/5bf160643b53699a28a8f0920c36d6ded767d249)

### v2.33 Release Notes

*Multi-Repository and GCS Support*

*Released April 5, 2021*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix option warnings breaking async `archive-get`/`archive-push`. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Reported by Lev Kokotov.*)

  An option warning in the async process was logged to a closed stdout, which made the process abort. Since the async role can now have different valid options than the default role, this happened more often. Option warnings are now logged only for the default role.

  Issue [#1299](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1299); Pull request [#1301](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1301); Commit [`3837e61`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3837e61a7504b1e211e4b3a70cbe97343c1cf2e6)
- Fix memory leak in backup during archive copy. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Reported by Christian ROUX, Efremov Egor.*)

  Copying WAL into the backup with `archive-copy` leaked memory, especially when the WAL needed to be recompressed.

  Issue [#1319](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1319); Commit [`778adbf`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/778adbf19f71ceb90071f01197bf189d59b14df0)
- Fix stack overflow in cipher passphrase generation. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Reported by bsiara.*)

  The buffer for the generated passphrase had no room for the terminating zero. This was found on arm64 with musl libc.

  Issue [#1294](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1294); Pull request [#1295](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1295); Commit [`d29855b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/d29855bd0bf8b957fa8c06445d22e425c443a704)
- Fix `repo-ls` `/` on S3 repositories. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Reported by Lesovsky Alexey.*)

  S3 reports `/` as a file without the expected headers, which caused an error. `/` is now reported as not existing on storage that does not support paths.

  Issue [#1317](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1317); Pull request [#1336](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1336); Commit [`b1d945e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b1d945ebb774c25676155af439b0558e8582bbe5)

**Features:**

- Multiple repository support. (*Contributed by Cynthia Shang, David Steele. Reviewed by Stefan Fercot, Stephen Frost.*)

  Up to four repositories may be configured, e.g. a local repository for fast restores and a remote repository for redundancy. `archive-push` pushes WAL to all repositories, and backups are scheduled for each repository. Some commands work with all repositories, while others require `repo`. `repo` is not required when only `repo1` is configured.

  [`3408f1e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3408f1ee2ee3f774699a15de045e0afe3eaf9a26) Enhance expire command multi-repo support.; Pull request [#1289](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1289)

  [`e28f6f1`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/e28f6f11e97a80e9c1adf1a6eaeeabbfc991914f) Expire continues if an error occurs processing a repository.; Pull request [#1308](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1308)

  [`bec3e20`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/bec3e20b2cb061ef0413df24234ebe246ea53063) Add archive-get command multi-repo support.; Pull request [#1292](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1292)

  [`118d9e6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/118d9e64fea71c2e049b52802e55f72f5793871b) Enhance restore command multi-repo support.; Pull request [#1300](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1300)

  [`065b2ff`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/065b2ff2300a9f06a8c32621fac521451ca942bd) Refactor info command repoMin/Max.

  [`0ddc038`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/0ddc0380ff408f9ed6e8bcd4d328df500681ce77) Remove restore default repo from integration tests.

  [`13dc8e6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/13dc8e68d74fa9e06180e67b7e59994767b4a34d) Make --repo optional for backup command.; Pull request [#1316](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1316)

  [`a1280c4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a1280c41e530500e5bbde73a900683f6db207686) Refactor archive-push command warnings to work like archive-get.

  [`3b8f0ef`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3b8f0ef7ae8a81ae969934fff4ad3da299454b24) Add write fault-tolerance to archive-push command.; Pull request [#1316](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1316)

  [`95063f6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/95063f6812279cd59380fd72e16e67b631582bd0) Make --repo optional for remaining commands except stanza-delete.

  [`1dbb3bf`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1dbb3bf50b94dcc9d53dfe1e5ad57a17bd6d38e8) Multiple repository support.; Pull request [#1227](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1227)

  [`31c7824`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/31c7824a4d84cbf5a0ba0938f4caa546fa67af0a) Allow stanza-* commands to be run remotely.; Pull request [#1326](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1326)

  [`2789d3b`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/2789d3b6206c4de9299a3bc459e04cdab793abc9) Improve info command fault tolerance.; Pull request [#1342](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1342)

  [`01b8e22`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/01b8e2258f6efa5386665c33cb5c267c154376fb) Improve archive-push command fault tolerance.; Pull request [#1343](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1343)
- GCS support for repository storage. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Tested by Daniel Farina.*)

  Set `repo-type=gcs` to store the repository on GCS or a GCS-compatible object store.

  [`dcb79ab`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/dcb79ab8fb18c63c2d56ecacd9d680b999ea044b) Decode JSON \u escaped characters.

  [`1b4b353`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/1b4b3538cc218f66ed1e903c3caeade4b0ab1120) Rename uri to path where appropriate in HTTP and storage modules.

  [`66a4ff4`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/66a4ff496afe63305cb1a437bcb52d1efc11edf7) Encode path before passing to HttpRequest.

  [`d485609`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/d4856096587c72daee76f8ad8408db677a16d6dd) Add strNewEncode(), strCatEncode(), and bufNewDecode().

  [`abcbe0f`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/abcbe0f9c1804ecb1b93b811cfb0a362c17e1b85) Combine encode module files into a single file.

  [`edab2a0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/edab2a0b89eabc5fd5ba6e795d938670bd5d3105) Use switch rather than if-else for encoding types.

  [`f6c3262`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/f6c3262861252b24e7e0cffd6d67e0c5e3291f3e) Do not expose valid/validate functions from encode module.

  [`a1f4fd3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/a1f4fd32a1790e569ccae3598cd9648bb25d13b6) Add ASSERT_MSG().

  [`00b60e5`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/00b60e564eef13f8adba0ab867fcde2c352669d6) Add base64url encoding.

  [`54c4eb0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/54c4eb0c10758879b4bf2b96efd2a1523ecccc21) Make remote storage objects writeable.

  [`088662d`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/088662d986288843d1bfd118b724091c93df4569) GCS support for repository storage.; Issue [#631](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/631); Issue [#743](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/743); Pull request [#1320](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1320)
- Add `archive-header-check` option. (*Reviewed by Stephen Frost, Cynthia Shang. Suggested by Hans-Jürgen Schönig.*)

  The WAL header is checked against the PostgreSQL version and system identifier to make sure WAL is pushed to the correct stanza. The check is enabled by default and can be disabled when needed, e.g. for encrypted WAL.

  Pull request [#1298](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1298); Commit [`b6106f3`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/b6106f3c1f586b410c50c9da8dcea583cc157423)

**Improvements:**

- Include recreated system databases during selective restore. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)

  A system database (`template0`, `template1`, or `postgres`) that was recreated has an OID that makes it look like a user database. These databases are now always restored, whatever their OID.

  Issue [#986](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/986); Pull request [#1307](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1307); Commit [`6942ff5`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6942ff569dd950a5146faa0bebfee69e241c4179)
- Exclude `content-length` from S3 signed headers. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Suggested by Brian P Bockelman.*)

  Some proxies modify the `content-length` header, which made authentication fail when it was signed.

  Issue [#1340](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1340); Pull request [#1341](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1341); Commit [`7d7ac0e`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/7d7ac0e0eb63a02bbc1dd8bd8fc89c6008c740f8)
- Consolidate less commonly used repository storage options. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)

  The S3 and Azure CA, host, port, and TLS verification options are renamed to `repo-storage-*`, e.g. `repo1-s3-host` is now `repo1-storage-host`. The old names are still accepted.

  Pull request [#1321](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1321); Commit [`d1aa765`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/d1aa765a9d5f290738c1de00c5ea99d5f6056ba5)
- Allow custom `config-path` default with `./configure --with-configdir`. (*Contributed by Michael Schout. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Packagers can set a different default for `config-path` when building. Without the option the default is still `/etc/pgbackrest`.

  [`6a717e0`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/6a717e032f1309ddc9dc955042b5e2ac2bc7324d) Set config path in configure script.

  [`9243962`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9243962b9504787c6f4af42318af6dc27094136d) Allow custom config-path default with ./configure --with-configdir.; Issue [#1251](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1251); Issue [#1293](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1293); Pull request [#1255](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1255)
- Log archive copy during `backup`. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang, Stefan Fercot.*)

  Issue [#1319](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1319); Pull request [#1328](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1328); Commit [`c862e96`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/c862e9654aff198167efeb897957bf93c1afe453)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Update reference to include links to user guide examples. (*Contributed by Cynthia Shang. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The command reference now links to the user guide sections with examples, and some descriptions were clarified, e.g. `set` for `expire`.

  Pull request [#1353](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1353); Commit [`d372dd6`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/d372dd652c9a01b72444998d87f0f44464676803)
- Update selective restore documentation with caveats. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang, Stefan Fercot.*)

  Recovery may fail unless `type=immediate` is specified, because PostgreSQL reports zeroed pages as errors after consistency is reached. On PostgreSQL >= 13 `ignore_invalid_pages` may be used, but check the logs for invalid pages in the selected databases.

  Issue [#796](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/796); Pull request [#1329](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1329); Commit [`92d12cc`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/92d12ccb9bd0787e30441794e2a2a4d4a4e0fe9c)
- Add `compress-type` clarification to `archive-copy` documentation. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang, Stefan Fercot.*)

  WAL must be recompressed when `archive-push` and `backup` use a different `compress-type`, which can be expensive.

  Issue [#1319](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1319); Pull request [#1327](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1327); Commit [`9506ffa`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/9506ffae39dd86d2c5454511026f1249097d96a2)
- Add `compress-level` defaults per `compress-type` value. (*Contributed by Cynthia Shang. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  The defaults are 9 for `bz2`, 6 for `gz`, 1 for `lz4`, and 3 for `zst`.

  Issue [#1242](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/1242); Pull request [#1346](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1346); Commit [`3e20608`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/3e206088e79c4a73a20b8f2fda1706c6b95fa188)
- Add note about required NFS settings being the same as PostgreSQL. (*Contributed by Cynthia Shang. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

  Issue [#871](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/issues/871); Pull request [#1347](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/pull/1347); Commit [`7598762`](https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest/commit/75987621fab5f57d0e95b42eabd301731587c438)

### v2.32 Release Notes

*Repository Commands*

*Released February 8, 2021*

**Bug Fixes:**

- Fix resume after partial delete of backup by prior resume. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Reported by Tom Swartz.*)

**Features:**

- Add `repo-ls` command. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang. Tested by Stefan Fercot.*)
- Add `repo-get` command. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot, David Steele. Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)
- Add `archive-mode-check` option. (*Contributed by Stefan Fercot. Reviewed by David Steele. Tested by Michael Banck.*)

**Improvements:**

- Improve `archive-get` performance. (*Reviewed by Cynthia Shang.*)

**Documentation Improvements:**

- Improve `expire` command documentation. (*Contributed by Cynthia Shang. Reviewed by David Steele.*)

### v2.31 Release Notes
